212:     private int dp(float value) {

219:     private boolean shouldShowTvLogo() {
220:         Configuration c = getResources().getConfiguration();
221: 
222:         boolean landscape =
223:                 c.orientation == Configuration.ORIENTATION_LANDSCAPE;
224: 
225:         boolean television =
226:                 (c.uiMode & Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_MASK)
227:                 == Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_TELEVISION;
228: 
229:         boolean tablet = c.smallestScreenWidthDp >= 600;
230: 
231:         return landscape && (television || tablet);
232:     }

236:         @JavascriptInterface
237:         public void play(
238:                 String name,
239:                 String url,
240:                 String headersJson,
241:                 String queueJson,
242:                 int selectedIndex) {
243: 
244:             runOnUiThread(() -> {
245:                 if (url == null ||
246:                         !url.matches("(?i)^https?://.*")) {
247:                     return;
248:                 }
249: 
250:                 Intent intent = new Intent(
251:                         MainActivity.this,
252:                         PlayerActivity.class
253:                 );
254: 
255:                 intent.putExtra("stream_url", url);
256:                 intent.putExtra(
257:                         "channel_name",
258:                         name == null ? "İSA TV HD" : name
259:                 );
260:                 intent.putExtra(
261:                         "headers_json",
262:                         headersJson == null ? "{}" : headersJson
263:                 );
264:                 
265: try {
266:     java.io.File queueFile = new java.io.File(
267:             getCacheDir(),
268:             "isa-player-queue.json"
269:     );
270: 
271:     try (java.io.FileOutputStream output =
272:                  new java.io.FileOutputStream(queueFile, false)) {
273:         output.write(
274:                 (queueJson == null ? "[]" : queueJson)
275:                         .getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
276:         );
277:         output.flush();
278:     }
279: 
280:     intent.putExtra("queue_file", queueFile.getAbsolutePath());
281:     intent.putExtra("selected_index", selectedIndex);
282:     startActivity(intent);
283: 
284: } catch (Exception e) {
285:     android.util.Log.e("ISATV", "Kanal kuyruğu aktarılamadı", e);
286: }
287: 
288:             });
289:         }

293:         @JavascriptInterface
294:         public void playIsaflix(
295:                 String name,
296:                 String url,
297:                 String headersJson,
298:                 String queueJson,
299:                 int selectedIndex) {
300: 
301:             runOnUiThread(() -> {
302:                 int targetIndex = selectedIndex;
303: 
304:                 if (url == null ||
305:                         !url.matches("(?i)^https?://.*")) {
306:                     return;
307:                 }
308: 
309:                 try {
310:                     org.json.JSONArray queue = new org.json.JSONArray(
311:                             queueJson == null ? "[]" : queueJson
312:                     );
313: 
314:                     if (queue.length() == 0) {
315:                         org.json.JSONObject one = new org.json.JSONObject();
316:                         one.put("name", name == null ? "İSAFLIX" : name);
317:                         one.put("url", url);
318:                         one.put("headers", new org.json.JSONObject(
319:                                 headersJson == null ? "{}" : headersJson
320:                         ));
321:                         one.put("is_live", false);
322:                         queue.put(one);
323:                         targetIndex = 0;
324:                     }
325: 
326:                     java.io.File queueFile = new java.io.File(
327:                             getCacheDir(),
328:                             "isaflix-player-queue.json"
329:                     );
330: 
331:                     try (java.io.FileOutputStream output =
332:                                  new java.io.FileOutputStream(queueFile, false)) {
333:                         output.write(queue.toString().getBytes(
334:                                 java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
335:                         ));
336:                         output.flush();
337:                     }
338: 
339:                     Intent intent = new Intent(
340:                             MainActivity.this,
341:                             IsaflixPlayerActivity.class
342:                     );
343:                     intent.putExtra("stream_url", url);
344:                     intent.putExtra(
345:                             "channel_name",
346:                             name == null ? "İSAFLIX" : name
347:                     );
348:                     intent.putExtra(
349:                             "headers_json",
350:                             headersJson == null ? "{}" : headersJson
351:                     );
352:                     intent.putExtra("queue_file", queueFile.getAbsolutePath());
353:                     intent.putExtra("selected_index", targetIndex);
354:                     startActivity(intent);
355: 
356:                 } catch (Exception e) {
357:                     Log.e(TAG, "İSAFLIX kuyruğu aktarılamadı", e);
358:                     Toast.makeText(
359:                             MainActivity.this,
360:                             "İSAFLIX oynatma listesi hazırlanamadı",
361:                             Toast.LENGTH_LONG
362:                     ).show();
363:                 }
364:             });
365:         }

372: 
373:         @JavascriptInterface
374:         public void setFavorite(String channelJson, boolean favorite) {
375:             try {
376:                 JSONObject channel = new JSONObject(channelJson);
377:                 String url = channel.optString("url", "");
378:                 if (url.isEmpty()) return;
379: 
380:                 android.content.SharedPreferences prefs =
381:                         getSharedPreferences("isa_tv_data", MODE_PRIVATE);
382: 
383:                 JSONArray old = new JSONArray(prefs.getString("favorites", "[]"));
384:                 JSONArray updated = new JSONArray();
385: 
386:                 for (int i = 0; i < old.length(); i++) {
387:                     JSONObject item = old.optJSONObject(i);
388:                     if (item == null) continue;
389:                     if (!url.equals(item.optString("url", ""))) {
390:                         updated.put(item);
391:                     }
392:                 }
393: 
394:                 if (favorite) updated.put(channel);
395: 
396:                 prefs.edit().putString("favorites", updated.toString()).apply();
397:             } catch (Exception e) {
398:                 Log.e(TAG, "Favori kaydedilemedi", e);
399:             }
400:         }

403:         private void reportCatalogProgress(
404:             String callbackId, String stage, int percent
405:         ) {
406:             final String id = callbackId == null
407:                 ? "0" : callbackId.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
408:             final int value = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent));
409: 
410:             runOnUiThread(() -> {
411:                 if (webView == null) return;
412:                 webView.evaluateJavascript(
413:                     "if(window.__isaCatalogProgress)window.__isaCatalogProgress(" +
414:                     JSONObject.quote(id) + "," +
415:                     JSONObject.quote(stage) + "," + value + ");",
416:                     null
417:                 );
418:             });
419:         }

421:         @JavascriptInterface
422:         public String readCatalogChunk(String offsetText, int requestedBytes) {
423:             try {
424:                 long offset = Long.parseLong(offsetText);
425: 
426:                 java.io.File file = new java.io.File(
427:                     getCacheDir(), "isaflix-web-katalog.json"
428:                 );
429: 
430:                 if (!file.isFile() || offset < 0 ||
431:                     offset >= file.length()) {
432:                     return "";
433:                 }
434: 
435:                 int size = Math.max(1024,
436:                     Math.min(65536, requestedBytes));
437: 
438:                 int count = (int)Math.min(
439:                     (long) size, file.length() - offset
440:                 );
441: 
442:                 byte[] buffer = new byte[count];
443: 
444:                 try (java.io.RandomAccessFile input =
445:                          new java.io.RandomAccessFile(file, "r")) {
446:                     input.seek(offset);
447:                     input.readFully(buffer);
448:                 }
449: 
450:                 return android.util.Base64.encodeToString(
451:                     buffer,
452:                     android.util.Base64.NO_WRAP
453:                 );
454: 
455:             } catch (Exception e) {
456:                 Log.e(TAG, "Catalog parça okuma hatası", e);
457:                 return "";
458:             }
459:         }

462:         @JavascriptInterface
463:         public void loadCachedCatalogCatalog(String callbackId) {
464:             final String id = callbackId == null
465:                 ? "0" : callbackId.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
466: 
467:             java.io.File cache = new java.io.File(
468:                 getCacheDir(), "isaflix-web-katalog.json"
469:             );
470: 
471:             final boolean exists =
472:                 cache.isFile() && cache.length() > 0;
473: 
474:             final long size = exists ? cache.length() : 0L;
475: 
476:             runOnUiThread(() -> {
477:                 if (webView == null) return;
478: 
479:                 if (exists) {
480:                     webView.evaluateJavascript(
481:                         "window.__isaCatalogReady(" +
482:                         JSONObject.quote(id) + "," +
483:                         size + ",null,false);",
484:                         null
485:                     );
486:                 } else {
487:                     webView.evaluateJavascript(
488:                         "window.__isaCatalogDone(" +
489:                         JSONObject.quote(id) +
490:                         ",null,'CACHE_EMPTY');",
491:                         null
492:                     );
493:                 }
494:             });
495:         }
496: 
497:         @JavascriptInterface
498:         public void fetchCatalogCatalog(String callbackId) {
499:             networkExecutor.execute(() -> {
500:                 String error = null;
501:                 boolean notModified = false;
502: 
503:                 HttpURLConnection connection = null;
504: 
505:                 try {
506:                     java.io.File cache = new java.io.File(
507:                         getCacheDir(), "isaflix-web-katalog.json"
508:                     );
509: 
510:                     android.content.SharedPreferences prefs =
511:                         getSharedPreferences("isaflix_web_catalog", MODE_PRIVATE);
512: 
513:                     String etag = prefs.getString("etag", "");
514:                     String modified = prefs.getString("modified", "");
515: 
516:                     URL url = new URL(
517:                         "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/thechampisher/ISA-TV-HD/main/INCELEME/isaflix-web-katalog.json"
518:                     );
519: 
520:                     connection = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
521:                     connection.setConnectTimeout(20000);
522:                     connection.setReadTimeout(180000);
523:                     connection.setRequestMethod("GET");
524:                     connection.setInstanceFollowRedirects(true);
525:                     connection.setRequestProperty("Accept-Encoding", "identity");
526:                     connection.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "ISA-TV-HD/1.0");
527: 
528:                     if (!etag.isEmpty())
529:                         connection.setRequestProperty("If-None-Match", etag);
530: 
531:                     if (!modified.isEmpty())
532:                         connection.setRequestProperty("If-Modified-Since", modified);
533: 
534:                     int status = connection.getResponseCode();
535: 
536:                     if (status == 304 && cache.isFile() && cache.length() > 0) {
537:                         notModified = true;
538:                     } else if (status >= 200 && status < 300) {
539:                         java.io.File encrypted = new java.io.File(
540:                             getCacheDir(), "catalog-catalog.download"
541:                         );
542: 
543:                         long totalBytes = connection.getContentLengthLong();
544:                         long downloaded = 0;
545:                         int lastPercent = -1;
546:                         long lastReport = 0;
547: 
548:                         reportCatalogProgress(callbackId, "download", 0);
549: 
550:                         try (
551:                             java.io.InputStream in = connection.getInputStream();
552:                             java.io.FileOutputStream out =
553:                                 new java.io.FileOutputStream(encrypted)
554:                         ) {
555:                             byte[] buffer = new byte[65536];
556:                             int n;
557: 
558:                             while ((n = in.read(buffer)) != -1) {
559:                                 out.write(buffer, 0, n);
560:                                 downloaded += n;
561: 
562:                                 int percent = totalBytes > 0
563:     ? (int)Math.min(70, downloaded * 70L / totalBytes)
564:     : 0;
565: 
566:                                 long now = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
567: 
568:                                 if (percent != lastPercent &&
569:                                     (now - lastReport >= 150 || percent == 100)) {
570:                                     reportCatalogProgress(
571:                                         callbackId, "download", percent
572:                                     );
573:                                     lastPercent = percent;
574:                                     lastReport = now;
575:                                 }
576:                             }
577: 
578:                             out.getFD().sync();
579:                         }
580: 
581:                         reportCatalogProgress(callbackId, "load", 70);
582: 
583:                         decryptCatalogFile(encrypted, cache, callbackId);
584:                         encrypted.delete();
585: 
586:                         String newEtag = connection.getHeaderField("ETag");
587:                         String newModified = connection.getHeaderField("Last-Modified");
588: 
589:                         android.content.SharedPreferences.Editor edit = prefs.edit();
590:                         if (newEtag != null) edit.putString("etag", newEtag);
591:                         if (newModified != null) edit.putString("modified", newModified);
592:                         edit.apply();
593: 
594:                     } else {
595:                         throw new java.io.IOException("Catalog HTTP " + status);
596:                     }
597: 
598:                 } catch (Exception ex) {
599:                     error = ex.getMessage() == null
600:                         ? "Catalog kataloğu alınamadı." : ex.getMessage();
601: 
602:                     Log.e(TAG, "Catalog katalog hatası", ex);
603:                 } finally {
604:                     if (connection != null) connection.disconnect();
605:                 }
606: 
607:                 final String failure = error;
608:                 final boolean finalNotModified = notModified;
609:                 final String id = callbackId == null
610:                     ? "0" : callbackId.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
611: 
612:                 final java.io.File catalogFile = new java.io.File(
613:                     getCacheDir(), "isaflix-web-katalog.json"
614:                 );
615: 
616:                 final long catalogSize =
617:                     failure == null && catalogFile.isFile()
618:                     ? catalogFile.length() : 0L;
619: 
620:                 runOnUiThread(() -> {
621:                     if (webView == null) return;
622: 
623:                     webView.evaluateJavascript(
624:                         "window.__isaCatalogReady(" + id + "," +
625:                         catalogSize + "," +
626:                         JSONObject.quote(failure) + "," +
627:                         (finalNotModified ? "true" : "false") + ");",
628:                         null
629:                     );
630:                 });
631:             });
632:         }
633: 
634:         private String readUtf8File(java.io.File file) throws Exception {
635:             StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder();
636:             try (
637:                 java.io.BufferedReader reader = new java.io.BufferedReader(
638:                     new java.io.InputStreamReader(
639:                         new java.io.FileInputStream(file),
640:                         StandardCharsets.UTF_8
641:                     ), 65536
642:                 )
643:             ) {
644:                 char[] buffer = new char[32768];
645:                 int n;
646:                 while ((n = reader.read(buffer)) != -1) {
647:                     result.append(buffer, 0, n);
648:                 }
649:             }
650:             return result.toString();
651:         }
652: 
653:         private void decryptCatalogFile(
654:             java.io.File source,
655:             java.io.File destination,
656:             String callbackId
657:         ) throws Exception {
658: 
659:             try (
660:                 java.io.BufferedInputStream input =
661:                     new java.io.BufferedInputStream(
662:                         new java.io.FileInputStream(source), 65536
663:                     )
664:             ) {
665:                 byte[] header = new byte[4];
666:                 int count = 0;
667:                 while (count < 4) {
668:                     int n = input.read(header, count, 4-count);
669:                     if (n < 0) throw new java.io.IOException("Dosya eksik.");
670:                     count += n;
671:                 }
672: 
673:                 boolean encrypted =
674:                     header[0] == 68 && header[1] == 84 &&
675:                     header[2] == 86 && header[3] == 49;
676: 
677:                 java.io.InputStream plain = input;
678: 
679:                 if (encrypted) {
680:                     byte[] iv = new byte[12];
681:                     int filled = 0;
682: 
683:                     while (filled < 12) {
684:                         int n = input.read(iv, filled, 12-filled);
685:                         if (n < 0)
686:                             throw new java.io.IOException("DTV1 IV eksik.");
687:                         filled += n;
688:                     }
689: 
690:                     byte[] digest = java.security.MessageDigest
691:                         .getInstance("SHA-256")
692:                         .digest(
693:                             (new String(new char[]{100,111,109,105,110,111}) + "|tv-cat|alog-v1|-2026")
694:                             .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
695:                         );
696: 
697:                     javax.crypto.Cipher cipher =
698:                         javax.crypto.Cipher.getInstance("AES/GCM/NoPadding");
699: 
700:                     cipher.init(
701:                         javax.crypto.Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE,
702:                         new javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec(digest, "AES"),
703:                         new javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec(128, iv)
704:                     );
705: 
706:                     final long cipherTotal = Math.max(1L, source.length() - 16L);
707: 
708:                     plain = new javax.crypto.CipherInputStream(
709:                         new java.io.FilterInputStream(input) {
710:                             private long consumed = 0;
711:                             private int lastPercent = -1;
712:                             private long lastReport = 0;
713: 
714:                             private void updateProgress(long amount) {
715:                                 consumed += amount;
716: 
717:                                 int percent = (int)Math.min(
718:                                     90L, 70L + consumed * 20L / cipherTotal
719:                                 );
720: 
721:                                 long now = android.os.SystemClock.elapsedRealtime();
722: 
723:                                 if (percent != lastPercent &&
724:                                     (now - lastReport >= 150 || percent == 99)) {
725:                                     reportCatalogProgress(
726:                                         callbackId, "load", percent
727:                                     );
728:                                     lastPercent = percent;
729:                                     lastReport = now;
730:                                 }
731:                             }
732: 
733:                             @Override
734:                             public int read(byte[] b, int off, int len)
735:                                 throws java.io.IOException {
736:                                 int n = super.read(b, off, len);
737:                                 if (n > 0) updateProgress(n);
738:                                 return n;
739:                             }
740: 
741:                             @Override
742:                             public int read() throws java.io.IOException {
743:                                 int value = super.read();
744:                                 if (value != -1) updateProgress(1);
745:                                 return value;
746:                             }
747:                         },
748:                         cipher
749:                     );
750:                 } else {
751:                     java.io.PushbackInputStream push =
752:                         new java.io.PushbackInputStream(input, 4);
753:                     push.unread(header);
754:                     plain = push;
755:                 }
756: 
757:                 java.io.File temp = new java.io.File(
758:                     destination.getParentFile(),
759:                     destination.getName() + ".tmp"
760:                 );
761: 
762:                 try (
763:                     java.io.InputStream decoded = plain;
764:                     java.io.BufferedOutputStream output =
765:                         new java.io.BufferedOutputStream(
766:                             new java.io.FileOutputStream(temp), 65536
767:                         )
768:                 ) {
769:                     byte[] buffer = new byte[65536];
770:                     int n;
771: 
772:                     while ((n = decoded.read(buffer)) != -1) {
773:                         output.write(buffer, 0, n);
774:                     }
775: 
776:                     output.flush();
777:                 }
778: 
779:                 reportCatalogProgress(callbackId, "load", 90);
780: 
781:                 if (!temp.renameTo(destination)) {
782:                     try (
783:                         java.io.InputStream in = new java.io.FileInputStream(temp);
784:                         java.io.OutputStream out =
785:                             new java.io.FileOutputStream(destination)
786:                     ) {
787:                         byte[] buffer = new byte[65536];
788:                         int n;
789:                         while ((n = in.read(buffer)) != -1)
790:                             out.write(buffer, 0, n);
791:                     }
792:                     temp.delete();
793:                 }
794:             }
795:         }
796: 
797:         @JavascriptInterface
798: public void fetchText(String address, String callbackId) {
799:     networkExecutor.execute(() -> {
800:         String result = null;
801:         String error = null;
802: 
803:         for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {
804:             HttpURLConnection connection = null;
805: 
806:             try {
807:                 URL url = new URL(address);
808: 
809:                 if (!"http".equalsIgnoreCase(url.getProtocol())
810:                         && !"https".equalsIgnoreCase(url.getProtocol())) {
811:                     throw new Exception("HTTP/HTTPS adresi gerekli.");
812:                 }
813: 
814:                 connection = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
815:                 connection.setConnectTimeout(20000);
816:                 connection.setReadTimeout(120000);
817:                 connection.setRequestMethod("GET");
818:                 connection.setInstanceFollowRedirects(true);
819:                 connection.setRequestProperty("Accept", "*/*");
820:                 connection.setRequestProperty("Accept-Encoding", "identity");
821:                 connection.setRequestProperty(
822:                     "User-Agent",
823:                     "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 11) AppleWebKit/537.36 "
824:                     + "(KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0 Mobile Safari/537.36"
825:                 );
826: 
827:                 int code = connection.getResponseCode();
828: 
829:                 if (code < 200 || code >= 300) {
830:                     throw new java.io.IOException("HTTP " + code);
831:                 }
832: 
833:                 StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
834: 
835:                 try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(
836:                         new InputStreamReader(
837:                             connection.getInputStream(),
838:                             StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
839: 
840:                     String line;
841:                     while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
842:                         output.append(line).append('\n');
843: 
844:                     if (output.length() > 64 * 1024 * 1024) {
845:                             throw new java.io.IOException("Kaynak 64 MB sınırını aştı.");
846:                         }
847: }
848:                 }
849: 
850:                 result = output.toString();
851:                 error = null;
852:                 break;
853: 
854:             } catch (Exception e) {
855:                 error = e.getMessage();
856:                 Log.w(TAG, "Kaynak indirme denemesi " + attempt + "/3", e);
857: 
858:                 if (attempt < 3) {
859:                     try {
860:                         Thread.sleep(attempt * 1200L);
861:                     } catch (InterruptedException interrupted) {
862:                         Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
863:                         error = "İşlem iptal edildi.";
864:                         break;
865:                     }
866:                 }
867:             } finally {
868:                 if (connection != null) {
869:                     connection.disconnect();
870:                 }
871:             }
872:         }
873: 
874:         final String response = result;
875:         final String failure = error;
876:         final String safeId = callbackId == null
877:                 ? "0"
878:                 : callbackId.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
879: 
880:         runOnUiThread(() -> {
881:             if (webView == null) return;
882: 
883:             String js = "window.__isaFetchDone("
884:                     + safeId + ","
885:                     + JSONObject.quote(response) + ","
886:                     + JSONObject.quote(failure) + ");";
887: 
888:             webView.evaluateJavascript(js, null);
889:         });
890:     });
891: }

896:         @JavascriptInterface
897:         public void fetchTextWithHeaders(String address, String headersJson, String callbackId) {
898:             networkExecutor.execute(() -> {
899:                 String result = null;
900:                 String error = null;
901:                 HttpURLConnection connection = null;
902:                 try {
903:                     URL url = new URL(address);
904:                     if (!"http".equalsIgnoreCase(url.getProtocol()) &&
905:                             !"https".equalsIgnoreCase(url.getProtocol())) {
906:                         throw new Exception("HTTP/HTTPS adresi gerekli.");
907:                     }
908:                     connection = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
909:                     connection.setConnectTimeout(20000);
910:                     connection.setReadTimeout(120000);
911:                     connection.setRequestMethod("GET");
912:                     connection.setInstanceFollowRedirects(true);
913:                     connection.setRequestProperty("Accept", "*/*");
914:                     connection.setRequestProperty("Accept-Encoding", "identity");
915: 
916:                     JSONObject supplied = new JSONObject(headersJson == null ? "{}" : headersJson);
917:                     java.util.Iterator<String> keys = supplied.keys();
918:                     while (keys.hasNext()) {
919:                         String key = keys.next();
920:                         String value = supplied.optString(key, "");
921:                         if (key.matches("(?i)[a-z0-9-]+") &&
922:                                 !key.matches("(?i)host|content-length|connection|transfer-encoding|accept-encoding") &&
923:                                 !value.contains("\r") && !value.contains("\n")) {
924:                             connection.setRequestProperty(key, value);
925:                         }
926:                     }
927: 
928:                     int code = connection.getResponseCode();
929:                     if (code < 200 || code >= 300) throw new java.io.IOException("HTTP " + code);
930: 
931:                     StringBuilder output = new StringBuilder();
932:                     try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(
933:                             new InputStreamReader(connection.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
934:                         String line;
935:                         while ((line = reader.readLine()) != null) {
936:                             output.append(line).append('\n');
937:                             if (output.length() > 64 * 1024 * 1024) {
938:                                 throw new Exception("Kaynak 64 MB sınırını aştı.");
939:                             }
940:                         }
941:                     }
942:                     result = output.toString();
943:                 } catch (Exception e) {
944:                     error = e.getMessage() == null ? "İstek başarısız." : e.getMessage();
945:                     Log.w(TAG, "Başlıklı kaynak isteği başarısız", e);
946:                 } finally {
947:                     if (connection != null) connection.disconnect();
948:                 }
949: 
950:                 final String response = result;
951:                 final String failure = error;
952:                 final String safeId = callbackId == null ? "0" : callbackId.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
953:                 runOnUiThread(() -> {
954:                     if (webView == null) return;
955:                     String callback = "window.__isaFetchDone(" + safeId + "," +
956:                             JSONObject.quote(response) + "," +
957:                             JSONObject.quote(failure) + ");";
958:                     webView.evaluateJavascript(callback, null);
959:                 });
960:             });
961:         }
962: 
963:                 @JavascriptInterface
964:         public void openPage(String address) {
965:             if (address == null || !(address.startsWith("https://") || address.startsWith("http://"))) return;
966:             runOnUiThread(() -> {
967:                 try {
968:                     Intent browser = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW, Uri.parse(address));
969:                     MainActivity.this.startActivity(browser);
970:                 } catch (Exception e) {
971:                     Log.e(TAG, "Kaynak sayfası açılamadı", e);
972:                 }
973:             });
974:         }

976: @JavascriptInterface
977:         public void cachePut(String key, String value) {
978:             try {
979:                 java.io.File directory = new java.io.File(
980:                         getFilesDir(),
981:                         "isa-source-cache"
982:                 );
983: 
984:                 if (!directory.exists()) {
985:                     directory.mkdirs();
986:                 }
987: 
988:                 String fileName = Integer.toHexString(
989:                         key == null ? 0 : key.hashCode()
990:                 ) + ".cache";
991: 
992:                 java.io.File file = new java.io.File(
993:                         directory,
994:                         fileName
995:                 );
996: 
997:                 try (java.io.FileOutputStream output =
998:                              new java.io.FileOutputStream(file, false)) {
999:                     output.write(
1000:                             (value == null ? "" : value)
1001:                                     .getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
1002:                     );
1003:                     output.flush();
1004:                 }
1005: 
1006:             } catch (Exception e) {
1007:                 android.util.Log.e("ISATV", "Önbellek yazma hatası", e);
1008:             }
1009:         }
1010: 
1011:         @JavascriptInterface
1012:         public void cacheGet(String key, String callbackId) {
1013:             String result = null;
1014:             String error = null;
1015: 
1016:             try {
1017:                 java.io.File directory = new java.io.File(
1018:                         getFilesDir(),
1019:                         "isa-source-cache"
1020:                 );
1021: 
1022:                 java.io.File file = new java.io.File(
1023:                         directory,
1024:                         Integer.toHexString(
1025:                                 key == null ? 0 : key.hashCode()
1026:                         ) + ".cache"
1027:                 );
1028: 
1029:                 if (file.isFile()) {
1030:                     StringBuilder content = new StringBuilder();
1031: 
1032:                     try (java.io.FileInputStream input =
1033:                                  new java.io.FileInputStream(file)) {
1034:                         byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
1035:                         int count;
1036: 
1037:                         while ((count = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
1038:                             content.append(
1039:                                     new String(
1040:                                             buffer,
1041:                                             0,
1042:                                             count,
1043:                                             java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
1044:                                     )
1045:                             );
1046:                         }
1047:                     }
1048: 
1049:                     result = content.toString();
1050:                 }
1051: 
1052:             } catch (Exception e) {
1053:                 error = e.getMessage();
1054:             }
1055: 
1056:             final String finalResult = result;
1057:             final String finalError = error;
1058: 
1059:             final String safeId = callbackId == null
1060:                     ? "0"
1061:                     : callbackId.replaceAll("[^0-9]", "");
1062: 
1063:             runOnUiThread(() -> {
1064:                 if (webView == null) return;
1065: 
1066:                 String valueJson = finalResult == null
1067:                         ? "null"
1068:                         : org.json.JSONObject.quote(finalResult);
1069: 
1070:                 String errorJson = finalError == null
1071:                         ? "null"
1072:                         : org.json.JSONObject.quote(finalError);
1073: 
1074:                 webView.evaluateJavascript(
1075:                         "window.__isaCacheDone(" +
1076:                                 safeId + "," +
1077:                                 valueJson + "," +
1078:                                 errorJson + ");",
1079:                         null
1080:                 );
1081:             });
1082:         }
1083: 
1084:     }

1086:     @Override
1087:     protected void onActivityResult(
1088:             int requestCode,
1089:             int resultCode,
1090:             Intent data) {
1091: 
1092:         super.onActivityResult(requestCode, resultCode, data);
1093: 
1094:         if (requestCode == FILE_REQUEST && fileCallback != null) {
1095:             Uri[] result =
1096:                     WebChromeClient.FileChooserParams.parseResult(
1097:                             resultCode,
1098:                             data
1099:                     );
1100: 
1101:             fileCallback.onReceiveValue(result);
1102:             fileCallback = null;
1103:         }
1104:     }
1105: 
1106:     @Override
1107:     protected void onResume() {
1108:         super.onResume();
1109:         if (webView != null) {
1110:             webView.postDelayed(() -> {
1111:                 if (webView != null) {
1112:                     webView.evaluateJavascript(
1113:                         "if(window.homepage && typeof window.homepage.restoreLastPlayedChannel === 'function'){" +
1114:                         "window.homepage.restoreLastPlayedChannel();}",
1115:                         null
1116:                     );
1117:                 }
1118:             }, 250);
1119:         }
1120:     }
1121: 
1122:     @Override
1123:     public void onBackPressed() {
1124:         if (webView == null) {
1125:             handleExitBack();
1126:             return;
1127:         }
1128: 
1129:         webView.evaluateJavascript(
1130:                 "(function(){return window.isaHandleBack ? window.isaHandleBack() : false;})()",
1131:                 value -> {
1132:                     if ("true".equals(value)) {
1133:                         backPressedAt = 0L;
1134:                     } else {
1135:                         handleExitBack();
1136:                     }
1137:                 }
1138:         );
1139:     }
1140: 
1141:     private void handleExitBack() {
1142:         long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
1143:         if (now - backPressedAt < 2000L) {
1144:             finish();
1145:             return;
1146:         }
1147: 
1148:         backPressedAt = now;
1149:         Toast.makeText(
1150:                 this,
1151:                 "Çıkmak için geri tuşuna tekrar basın",
1152:                 Toast.LENGTH_SHORT
1153:         ).show();
1154:     }
1155: 
1156:     @Override
1157:     protected void onDestroy() {
1158:         if (fileCallback != null) {
1159:             fileCallback.onReceiveValue(null);
1160:             fileCallback = null;
1161:         }
1162: 
1163:         networkExecutor.shutdownNow();
1164: 
1165:         if (webView != null) {
1166:             webView.destroy();
1167:             webView = null;
1168:         }
1169: 
1170:         super.onDestroy();
1171:     }
1172: }

177:                                 !key.matches("(?i)host|content-length|connection|transfer-encoding|accept-encoding") &&
178:                                 !value.contains("\r") && !value.contains("\n")) {

231:     private void applyImmersiveMode() {
232:         View decor = getWindow().getDecorView();
233:         decor.setSystemUiVisibility(
234:             View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_IMMERSIVE_STICKY
235:             | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_FULLSCREEN
236:             | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_HIDE_NAVIGATION
237:             | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_FULLSCREEN
238:             | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_HIDE_NAVIGATION
239:             | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_STABLE
240:         );
241:     }
242: 
243:     @Override
244:     public void onWindowFocusChanged(boolean hasFocus) {
245:         super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus);
246:         if (hasFocus) applyImmersiveMode();
247:     }
248: 
249: 
250:     @Override
251:     public boolean dispatchKeyEvent(android.view.KeyEvent event) {
252: 
253:         int key = event.getKeyCode();
254: 
255:         boolean next =
256:                 key == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_UP ||
257:                 key == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_CHANNEL_UP;
258: 
259:         boolean previous =
260:                 key == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_DOWN ||
261:                 key == android.view.KeyEvent.KEYCODE_CHANNEL_DOWN;
262: 
263:         if (next || previous) {
264: 
265:             if (event.getAction() == android.view.KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) {
266: 
267:                 if (event.getRepeatCount() == 0) {
268:                     finishChannelSwipe(next ? 1 : -1);
269:                 }
270:             }
271: 
272:             return true;
273:         }
274: 
275:         return super.dispatchKeyEvent(event);
276:     }
277: 
278:     private void createInterface() {
279:         FrameLayout root = new FrameLayout(this);
280:         root.setBackgroundColor(Color.BLACK);
281: 
282:         playerView = new PlayerView(this);
283:         playerView.setUseController(false);
284: 
285:         root.addView(playerView, new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
286:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
287:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
288:         ));
289: 
290:         // Ekrana dokunulduğunda yalnızca kanal listesi açılır.
291:         // Media3 oynatma kontrolleri devre dışıdır.
292:         View channelTapLayer = new View(this);
293:         channelTapLayer.setBackgroundColor(Color.TRANSPARENT);
294:         channelTapLayer.setOnTouchListener(new View.OnTouchListener() {
295:             private float startX;
296:             private float startY;
297:             private boolean dragging;
298:             private boolean horizontalDrag;
299:             private boolean ignoreHorizontal;
300: 
301:             @Override
302:             public boolean onTouch(View v, android.view.MotionEvent event) {
303:                 boolean portrait =
304:                         getResources().getConfiguration().orientation
305:                         == Configuration.ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT;
306: 
307:                 switch (event.getAction()) {
308:                     case android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN:
309:                         startX = event.getX();
310:                         startY = event.getY();
311:                         dragging = false;
312:                         horizontalDrag = false;
313:                         ignoreHorizontal = false;
314:                         return true;
315: 
316:                     case android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE:
317:                         if (channelTransitioning) return true;
318: 
319:                         float dx = event.getX() - startX;
320:                         float dy = event.getY() - startY;
321: 
322:                         if (Math.abs(dx) > dp(10) && Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) {
323:                             dragging = true;
324:                             horizontalDrag = true;
325:                             float width = Math.max(1, playerView.getWidth());
326:                             float offset = Math.max(-width, Math.min(width, dx));
327:                             playerView.setTranslationX(offset);
328:                             if (portraitPanel != null) portraitPanel.setTranslationX(offset);
329:                         } else if (Math.abs(dy) > dp(8) && Math.abs(dy) > Math.abs(dx)) {
330:                             dragging = true;
331:                             horizontalDrag = false;
332:                             float height = Math.max(playerView.getHeight(), getResources().getDisplayMetrics().heightPixels);
333:                             float offset = Math.max(-height, Math.min(height, dy));
334:                             playerView.setTranslationY(offset);
335:                             if (portraitPanel != null) portraitPanel.setTranslationY(offset);
336:                         }
337:                         if (dragging) {
338:                             handler.removeCallbacks(hideChannelButton);
339:                             channelButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
340:                         }
341:                         return true;
342: 
343:                     case android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_UP:
344: 
345:                         float moveY = event.getY() - startY;
346:                         float moveX = event.getX() - startX;
347: 
348:                         if (dragging) {
349:                             if (horizontalDrag) {
350:                                 float threshold = Math.max(dp(70), playerView.getWidth() * 0.18f);
351:                                 if (Math.abs(moveX) >= threshold) finishChannelSwipe(moveX < 0 ? 1 : -1, true);
352:                                 else animateViewsBack(true);
353:                             } else {
354:                                 float threshold = Math.max(dp(90), playerView.getHeight() * 0.22f);
355:                                 if (Math.abs(moveY) >= threshold) finishChannelSwipe(moveY < 0 ? 1 : -1, false);
356:                                 else animateViewsBack(false);
357:                             }
358:                         } else if (Math.abs(moveX) < dp(30) &&
359:                                    Math.abs(moveY) < dp(30)) {
360:                             // Dokunma ile kanal listesi açılmaz.
361:                             // Kanal değiştirme kaydırma hareketi korunur.
362:                             channelButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
363:                         }
364: 
365:                         dragging = false;
366:                         horizontalDrag = false;
367:                         return true;
368: 
369:                     case android.view.MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL:
370:                         if (dragging) animateViewsBack(horizontalDrag);
371:                         dragging = false;
372:                         horizontalDrag = false;
373:                         return true;
374:                 }
375:                 return true;
376:             }
377:         });
378: 
379:         root.addView(channelTapLayer, new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
380:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
381:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
382:         ));
383: 
384:         status = new TextView(this);
385:         status.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
386:         status.setTextSize(15);
387:         status.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
388:         status.setBackgroundColor(0x99000000);
389:         status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
390: 
391:         root.addView(status, new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
392:                 -2, -2, Gravity.CENTER
393:         ));
394: 
395:         loadingSpinner = new android.widget.ProgressBar(
396:                 this, null, android.R.attr.progressBarStyleLarge
397:         );
398:         loadingSpinner.setIndeterminate(true);
399:         loadingSpinner.setIndeterminateTintList(
400:                 android.content.res.ColorStateList.valueOf(Color.RED)
401:         );
402:         loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
403: 
404:         FrameLayout.LayoutParams spinnerParams =
405:                 new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dp(54), dp(54), Gravity.CENTER);
406:         root.addView(loadingSpinner, spinnerParams);
407: 
408:         channelButton = new Button(this);
409:         channelButton.setText("KANAL LİSTESİ");
410:         channelButton.setTextSize(12);
411:         channelButton.setAllCaps(false);
412: 
413:         channelButton.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
414:         channelButton.setPadding(dp(18), dp(8), dp(18), dp(8));
415: 
416:         android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable buttonBackground =
417:             new android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable();
418:         buttonBackground.setColor(0xCC18263A);
419:         buttonBackground.setCornerRadius(dp(24));
420:         buttonBackground.setStroke(dp(1), 0x66FFFFFF);
421: 
422:         channelButton.setBackground(buttonBackground);
423:         channelButton.setElevation(dp(8));
424:         channelButton.setOnClickListener(v -> showChannelList());
425:         channelButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
426: 
427:         FrameLayout.LayoutParams buttonParams =
428:                 new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
429:                         -2, dp(46),
430:                         Gravity.TOP | Gravity.LEFT
431:                 );
432: 
433:         buttonParams.setMargins(dp(16), dp(18), 0, 0);
434:         root.addView(channelButton, buttonParams);
435: 
436:         if (shouldShowTvLogo()) {
437:             try {
438:                 logo = new ImageView(this);
439: 
440:                 try (java.io.InputStream input =
441:                         getAssets().open("isa-tv-hd/assets/logo.png")) {
442:                     logo.setImageBitmap(
443:                             BitmapFactory.decodeStream(input)
444:                     );
445:                 }
446: 
447:                 boolean portraitLogo =
448:                         getResources().getConfiguration().orientation == 1;
449: 
450:                 logo.setScaleType(ImageView.ScaleType.FIT_CENTER);
451:                 logo.setPadding(
452:                         portraitLogo ? 0 : dp(5),
453:                         portraitLogo ? 0 : dp(5),
454:                         portraitLogo ? 0 : dp(5),
455:                         portraitLogo ? 0 : dp(5));
456:                 logo.setBackgroundColor(
457:                         portraitLogo ? Color.TRANSPARENT : 0x880F0F0F);
458:                 logo.setContentDescription("İSA TV HD");
459: 
460:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams logoParams =
461:                         new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
462:                                 portraitLogo ? dp(68) : dp(130),
463:                                 portraitLogo ? dp(42) : dp(80),
464:                                 Gravity.TOP | Gravity.RIGHT);
465:                 logoParams.setMargins(
466:                         0,
467:                         portraitLogo ? dp(8) : dp(12),
468:                         portraitLogo ? dp(8) : dp(12),
469:                         0);
470: 
471:                 View videoContent = playerView.getChildAt(0);
472:                 if (videoContent instanceof FrameLayout) {
473:                     ((FrameLayout) videoContent).addView(logo, logoParams);
474:                     // Yayının sol alt köşesindeki reklam/QR işaretini kapat.
475:                 } else {
476:                     root.addView(logo, logoParams);
477:                 }
478: 
479:             } catch (Exception e) {
480:                 Log.e(TAG, "Logo yüklenemedi", e);
481:             }
482:         }
483:

485:         portraitPanel = new LinearLayout(this);

496:         portraitChannelName.setTextSize(18);
497:         portraitChannelName.setTypeface(null, android.graphics.Typeface.BOLD);
498:         portraitChannelName.setMaxLines(4);

500:

512:         liveBadgeBg.setColor(0xFF18263A);

591:     
592:     private void initializePlayer() {
593:         player = createManagedPlayer(false);
594:         playerView.setPlayer(player);
595:     }
596: 
597: 
598:     private ExoPlayer createManagedPlayer(boolean preload) {
599:         DefaultLoadControl loadControl =
600:                 new DefaultLoadControl.Builder()
601:                         .setBufferDurationsMs(
602:                                 15000,
603:                                 60000,
604:                                 1000,
605:                                 3000
606:                         )
607:                         .setPrioritizeTimeOverSizeThresholds(true)
608:                         .build();
609: 
610:         ExoPlayer p = new ExoPlayer.Builder(this)
611:                 .setLoadControl(loadControl)
612:                 .build();
613: 
614:         p.setVolume(preload ? 0f : 1f);
615: 
616:         p.setTrackSelectionParameters(
617:                 p.getTrackSelectionParameters()
618:                         .buildUpon()
619:                         .setMaxVideoBitrate(
620:                                 preload ? 1500000 : Integer.MAX_VALUE
621:                         )
622:                         .build()
623:         );
624: 
625:         Handler retryHandler = new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper());
626:         final int[] retryCount = {0};
627: 
628:         Runnable[] retryTask = new Runnable[1];
629: 
630:         retryTask[0] = () -> {
631:             if (p != player || p.isReleased()) return;
632: 
633:             if (retryCount[0] >= 5) {
634:                 Log.e(TAG, "Canlı yayın yeniden bağlanma denemeleri tükendi.");
635:                 return;
636:             }
637: 
638:             retryCount[0]++;
639:             Log.w(TAG, "Yayın yeniden hazırlanıyor. Deneme: "
640:                     + retryCount[0]);
641: 
642:             p.prepare();
643:             p.play();
644:         };
645: 
646:         p.addListener(new Player.Listener() {
647:             @Override
648:             public void onPlaybackStateChanged(int state) {
649:                 if (p != player) return;
650: 
651:                 if (state == Player.STATE_READY) {
652:                     retryCount[0] = 0;
653:                     retryHandler.removeCallbacks(retryTask[0]);
654:                     status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
655:                     loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
656: 
657:                 } else if (state == Player.STATE_BUFFERING) {
658:                     loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
659: 
660:                 } else if (state == Player.STATE_IDLE) {
661:                     loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
662: 
663:                 } else if (state == Player.STATE_ENDED) {
664:                     MediaItem current = p.getCurrentMediaItem();
665: 
666:                     if (current != null
667:                             && current.localConfiguration != null) {
668:                         String uri = current.localConfiguration.uri
669:                                 .toString().toLowerCase();
670: 
671:                         if (uri.contains(".m3u8")) {
672:                             retryHandler.removeCallbacks(retryTask[0]);
673:                             retryHandler.postDelayed(retryTask[0], 1500);
674:                         }
675:                     }
676:                 }
677:             }
678: 
679:             @Override
680:             public void onPlayerError(PlaybackException error) {
681:                 Log.e(TAG, "Oynatıcı hatası: "
682:                         + error.getErrorCodeName(), error);
683: 
684:                 if (p == player) {
685:                     status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
686:                     loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
687: 
688:                     retryHandler.removeCallbacks(retryTask[0]);
689: 
690:                     long delay = Math.min(
691:                             1500L * (retryCount[0] + 1),
692:                             10000L
693:                     );
694: 
695:                     retryHandler.postDelayed(retryTask[0], delay);
696:                 }
697:             }
698:         });
699: 
700:         return p;
701:     }
702: 
703:     private void prepareChannel(ExoPlayer target, int index,
704:                                 boolean preload) {
705:         if (index < 0 || index >= channels.size()) return;
706: 
707:         Channel channel = channels.get(index);
708: 
709:         try {
710:             DefaultHttpDataSource.Factory factory =
711:                     new DefaultHttpDataSource.Factory()
712:                             .setUserAgent(
713:                                     channel.headers.containsKey("User-Agent")
714:                                     ? channel.headers.get("User-Agent")
715:                                     : "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 11) "
716:                                     + "AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/120 Safari/537.36"
717:                             )
718:                             .setConnectTimeoutMs(20000)
719:                             .setReadTimeoutMs(60000)
720:                             .setAllowCrossProtocolRedirects(true)
721:                             .setDefaultRequestProperties(channel.headers);
722: 
723:             String url = channel.url;
724:             String lowerUrl = url.toLowerCase();
725:             boolean isHls = lowerUrl.contains(".m3u8");
726: 
727:             MediaItem.Builder itemBuilder = new MediaItem.Builder()
728:                     .setUri(Uri.parse(url))
729:                     .setMediaId(channel.name == null
730:                             ? url : channel.name);
731: 
732:             if (isHls) {
733:                 itemBuilder.setLiveConfiguration(
734:                         new MediaItem.LiveConfiguration.Builder()
735:                                 .setTargetOffsetMs(15000)
736:                                 .setMinPlaybackSpeed(0.97f)
737:                                 .setMaxPlaybackSpeed(1.03f)
738:                                 .build()
739:                 );
740:             }
741: 
742:             MediaItem item = itemBuilder.build();
743: 
744:             target.stop();
745:             target.clearMediaItems();
746: 
747:             target.setVolume(preload ? 0f : 1f);
748: 
749:             target.setTrackSelectionParameters(
750:                     target.getTrackSelectionParameters()
751:                             .buildUpon()
752:                             .setMaxVideoBitrate(
753:                                     preload ? 1500000 : Integer.MAX_VALUE
754:                             )
755:                             .build()
756:             );
757: 
758:             androidx.media3.exoplayer.source.MediaSource mediaSource;
759: 
760:             if (isHls) {
761:                 mediaSource = new HlsMediaSource.Factory(factory)
762:                         .setAllowChunklessPreparation(true)
763:                         .createMediaSource(item);
764:             } else {
765:                 mediaSource =
766:                         new androidx.media3.exoplayer.source.DefaultMediaSourceFactory(
767:                                 factory
768:                         ).createMediaSource(item);
769:             }
770: 
771:             target.setMediaSource(mediaSource);
772:             target.prepare();
773:             target.setPlayWhenReady(!preload);
774: 
775:         } catch (Exception e) {
776:             Log.e(TAG, "Kanal hazırlanamadı: " + index, e);
777:         }
778:     }
779: 
780:     
781: 
782:     private ExoPlayer obtainStandbyPlayer() {
783:         if (!standbyPlayers.isEmpty()) {
784:             return standbyPlayers.remove(standbyPlayers.size() - 1);
785:         }
786: 
787:         return createManagedPlayer(true);
788:     }
789: 
790:     private void recyclePlayer(ExoPlayer p) {
791:         if (p == null || p == player) return;
792: 
793:         p.setPlayWhenReady(false);
794:         p.stop();
795:         p.clearMediaItems();
796:         p.setVolume(0f);
797: 
798:         if (!standbyPlayers.contains(p) && standbyPlayers.size() < 2) {
799:             standbyPlayers.add(p);
800:         } else if (!standbyPlayers.contains(p)) {
801:             p.release();
802:         }
803:     }
804: 
805:     private void prepareNeighborChannels() {
806:         if (channels.size() < 2 || player == null) return;
807: 
808:         int previous = (selectedIndex - 1 + channels.size())
809:                 % channels.size();
810: 
811:         int next = (selectedIndex + 1) % channels.size();
812: 
813:         java.util.HashSet<Integer> wanted = new java.util.HashSet<>();
814:         wanted.add(previous);
815:         wanted.add(next);
816:         wanted.remove(selectedIndex);
817: 
818:         java.util.Iterator<Map.Entry<Integer, ExoPlayer>> iterator =
819:                 preloadedPlayers.entrySet().iterator();
820: 
821:         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
822:             Map.Entry<Integer, ExoPlayer> entry = iterator.next();
823: 
824:             if (!wanted.contains(entry.getKey())) {
825:                 ExoPlayer old = entry.getValue();
826:                 iterator.remove();
827:                 recyclePlayer(old);
828:             }
829:         }
830: 
831:         for (Integer index : wanted) {
832:             if (!preloadedPlayers.containsKey(index)) {
833:                 ExoPlayer standby = obtainStandbyPlayer();
834: 
835:                 preloadedPlayers.put(index, standby);
836:                 prepareChannel(standby, index, true);
837:             }
838:         }
839:     }
840: 
841: 
842:     private void animateViewsBack(boolean horizontal) {
843:         if (playerView == null) return;
844: 
845:         playerView.animate().cancel();
846:         playerView.animate()
847:                 .translationX(horizontal ? 0f : playerView.getTranslationX())
848:                 .translationY(horizontal ? playerView.getTranslationY() : 0f)
849:                 .setDuration(180)
850:                 .start();
851: 
852:         if (portraitPanel != null) {
853:             portraitPanel.animate().cancel();
854:             portraitPanel.animate()
855:                     .translationX(horizontal ? 0f : portraitPanel.getTranslationX())
856:                     .translationY(horizontal ? portraitPanel.getTranslationY() : 0f)
857:                     .setDuration(180)
858:                     .start();
859:         }
860:     }
861: 
862:     private void finishChannelSwipe(int direction) {
863:         finishChannelSwipe(direction, false);
864:     }
865: 
866:     private void finishChannelSwipe(int direction, boolean horizontal) {
867:         if (channels.isEmpty() || channelTransitioning ||
868:                 playerView == null) return;
869: 
870:         channelTransitioning = true;
871: 
872:         final int distance = horizontal ? Math.max(1, playerView.getWidth()) : Math.max(
873:                 playerView.getHeight(), getResources().getDisplayMetrics().heightPixels);
874:         final float exit = direction > 0 ? -distance : distance;
875:         final float enter = -exit;
876: 
877:         playerView.animate().cancel();
878: 
879:         if (portraitPanel != null) {
880:             portraitPanel.animate().cancel();
881:         }
882: 
883:         playerView.animate()
884:                 .translationX(horizontal ? exit : 0f)
885:                 .translationY(horizontal ? 0f : exit)
886:                 .setDuration(150)
887:                 .withEndAction(() -> {
888:                     selectedIndex += direction;
889: 
890:                     if (selectedIndex >= channels.size()) selectedIndex = 0;
891:                     if (selectedIndex < 0) selectedIndex = channels.size() - 1;
892: 
893:                     playSelected();
894: 
895:                     playerView.setTranslationX(horizontal ? enter : 0f);
896:                     playerView.setTranslationY(horizontal ? 0f : enter);
897: 
898:                     if (portraitPanel != null) {
899:                         portraitPanel.setTranslationX(horizontal ? enter : 0f);
900:                         portraitPanel.setTranslationY(horizontal ? 0f : enter);
901:                     }
902: 
903:                     playerView.animate()
904:                             .translationX(0f)
905:                             .translationY(0f)
906:                             .setDuration(220)
907:                             .withEndAction(() -> {
908:                                 channelTransitioning = false;
909:                             })
910:                             .start();
911: 
912:                     if (portraitPanel != null) {
913:                         portraitPanel.animate()
914:                                 .translationX(0f)
915:                                 .translationY(0f)
916:                                 .setDuration(220)
917:                                 .start();
918:                     }
919:                 })
920:                 .start();
921: 
922:         if (portraitPanel != null) {
923:             portraitPanel.animate()
924:                     .translationX(horizontal ? exit : 0f)
925:                     .translationY(horizontal ? 0f : exit)
926:                     .setDuration(150)
927:                     .start();
928:         }
929:     }
930: 
931:     
932:     private void playSelected() {
933:         if (player == null || selectedIndex < 0 ||
934:                 selectedIndex >= channels.size()) return;
935: 
936:         Channel channel = channels.get(selectedIndex);
937: 
938:         updatePortraitOverlay();
939: 
940:         if (loadingSpinner != null) {
941:             loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
942:         }
943: 
944:         if (status != null) {
945:             status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
946:         }
947: 
948:         ExoPlayer oldActive = player;
949:         ExoPlayer readyPlayer = preloadedPlayers.remove(selectedIndex);
950: 
951:         try {
952:             if (readyPlayer != null) {
953:                 // Hazırlanmış komşu oynatıcıyı aktif hale getir.
954:                 player = readyPlayer;
955:                 playerView.setPlayer(player);
956: 
957:                 player.setVolume(1f);
958:                 player.setTrackSelectionParameters(
959:                         player.getTrackSelectionParameters()
960:                                 .buildUpon()
961:                                 .setMaxVideoBitrate(Integer.MAX_VALUE)
962:                                 .build()
963:                 );
964: 
965:                 player.setPlayWhenReady(true);
966: 
967:                 recyclePlayer(oldActive);
968: 
969:             } else {
970:                 // Hazır değilse mevcut aktif oynatıcıyla aç.
971:                 prepareChannel(player, selectedIndex, false);
972:             }
973: 
974:             setTitle(channel.name);
975:             status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
976: 
977:             // Önceden hazırlanmış oynatıcı READY olmuş olabilir.
978:             int activeState = player.getPlaybackState();
979: 
980:             if (activeState == Player.STATE_READY) {
981:                 loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
982:             } else if (activeState == Player.STATE_BUFFERING ||
983:                        activeState == Player.STATE_IDLE) {
984:                 loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
985:             }
986: 
987:             applyPreloadPolicy();
988: 
989:         } catch (Exception e) {
990:             Log.e(TAG, "Kanal başlatılamadı", e);
991:             loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
992:         }
993:     }
994: 
995:
