1000: private boolean isPortraitMode() {
1001:     return getResources().getConfiguration().orientation
1002:             == Configuration.ORIENTATION_PORTRAIT;
1003: }
1004: 
1005: private boolean isTelevisionDevice() {
1006:     int type = getResources().getConfiguration().uiMode
1007:             & Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_MASK;
1008: 
1009:     return type == Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_TELEVISION
1010:             || getPackageManager().hasSystemFeature(
1011:                     android.content.pm.PackageManager.FEATURE_LEANBACK
1012:             );
1013: }
1014: 
1015: private boolean isPhoneDevice() {
1016:     if (isTelevisionDevice()) return false;
1017: 
1018:     int smallestWidth =
1019:             getResources().getConfiguration().smallestScreenWidthDp;
1020: 
1021:     if (smallestWidth > 0) {
1022:         return smallestWidth < 600;
1023:     }
1024: 
1025:     float density = getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
1026:     float widthDp =
1027:             getResources().getDisplayMetrics().widthPixels / density;
1028: 
1029:     return widthDp < 600;
1030: }
1031: 
1032: private void stopNeighborPreloads() {
1033:     Iterator<Map.Entry<Integer, ExoPlayer>> iterator =
1034:             preloadedPlayers.entrySet().iterator();
1035: 
1036:     while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1037:         ExoPlayer standby = iterator.next().getValue();
1038:         iterator.remove();
1039:         recyclePlayer(standby);
1040:     }
1041: }
1042: 
1043: 
1044:     // Komşu yayınları aç ve altı saniyelik sayaç başlat.
1045:     private void startNeighborPreloadsWithTimeout() {
1046:         handler.removeCallbacks(neighborPreloadTimeout);
1047: 
1048:         neighborPreloadUnlocked = true;
1049: 
1050:         if (portraitWarning != null) {
1051:             portraitWarning.setVisibility(View.GONE);
1052:         }
1053: 
1054:         if (player != null && channels.size() >= 2) {
1055:             prepareNeighborChannels();
1056:         } else {
1057:             stopNeighborPreloads();
1058:         }
1059: 
1060:         handler.postDelayed(neighborPreloadTimeout, 6000L);
1061:     }
1062: 
1063: private void applyPreloadPolicy() {
1064:         startNeighborPreloadsWithTimeout();
1065:     }
1066: 
1067: private boolean isCurrentChannelFavorite() {
1068:     if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= channels.size()) return false;
1069: 
1070:     String url = channels.get(selectedIndex).url;
1071:     try {
1072:         JSONArray favorites = new JSONArray(
1073:                 getSharedPreferences("isa_tv_data", MODE_PRIVATE)
1074:                         .getString("favorites", "[]"));
1075: 
1076:         for (int i = 0; i < favorites.length(); i++) {
1077:             JSONObject item = favorites.optJSONObject(i);
1078:             if (item != null && url.equals(item.optString("url", ""))) {
1079:                 return true;
1080:             }
1081:         }
1082:     } catch (Exception e) {
1083:         Log.e(TAG, "Favoriler okunamadı", e);
1084:     }
1085:     return false;
1086: }
1087: 
1088: private void updatePortraitOverlay() {
1089:     if (portraitChannelName == null || favoriteButton == null) return;
1090: 
1091:     boolean portrait = isPortraitMode();
1092:     if (portraitPanel != null) portraitPanel.setVisibility(portrait ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
1093: 
1094:     portraitChannelName.setVisibility(portrait ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
1095:     boolean liveItem = selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < channels.size()
1096:             && channels.get(selectedIndex).isLive;
1097:     portraitLiveBadge.setVisibility(portrait && liveItem ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
1098:     if (portraitEpisodeInfo != null) portraitEpisodeInfo.setVisibility(portrait ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
1099:     favoriteButton.setVisibility(portrait ? View.VISIBLE : View.GONE);
1100: 
1101:     if (!portrait) return;
1102: 
1103:     if (selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < channels.size()) {
1104:         Channel channel = channels.get(selectedIndex);
1105:         portraitChannelName.setText(
1106:                 channel.name == null ? "İSA TV HD" : channel.name);
1107:         if (portraitEpisodeInfo != null) {
1108:             if (channel.season > 0 || channel.episode > 0) {
1109:                 String label = (channel.season > 0 ? "Sezon " + channel.season : "") +
1110:                         (channel.episode > 0 ? "  ·  " + channel.episode + ". Bölüm" : "");
1111:                 portraitEpisodeInfo.setText(label.trim());
1112:                 portraitEpisodeInfo.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
1113:             } else portraitEpisodeInfo.setVisibility(View.GONE);
1114:         }
1115:     }
1116: 
1117:     favoriteButton.setText(isCurrentChannelFavorite() ? "♥" : "♡");
1118:     favoriteButton.setContentDescription(
1119:             isCurrentChannelFavorite()
1120:                     ? "Favorilerden çıkar"
1121:                     : "Favorilere ekle");
1122: }
1123: 
1124: private void toggleCurrentFavorite() {
1125:     if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= channels.size()) return;
1126: 
1127:     Channel channel = channels.get(selectedIndex);
1128:     boolean add = !isCurrentChannelFavorite();
1129: 
1130:     try {
1131:         JSONObject item = new JSONObject();
1132:         item.put("name", channel.name == null ? "İsimsiz kanal" : channel.name);
1133:         item.put("url", channel.url);
1134:         item.put("group", channel.group == null ? "Genel" : channel.group);
1135:         item.put("logo", channel.logo == null ? "" : channel.logo);
1136:         item.put("is_live", channel.isLive);
1137:         item.put("season", channel.season);
1138:         item.put("episode", channel.episode);
1139: 
1140:         android.content.SharedPreferences prefs =
1141:                 getSharedPreferences("isa_tv_data", MODE_PRIVATE);
1142: 
1143:         JSONArray old = new JSONArray(prefs.getString("favorites", "[]"));
1144:         JSONArray updated = new JSONArray();
1145: 
1146:         for (int i = 0; i < old.length(); i++) {
1147:             JSONObject existing = old.optJSONObject(i);
1148:             if (existing == null) continue;
1149: 
1150:             if (!channel.url.equals(existing.optString("url", ""))) {
1151:                 updated.put(existing);
1152:             }
1153:         }
1154: 
1155:         if (add) updated.put(item);
1156: 
1157:         prefs.edit().putString("favorites", updated.toString()).apply();
1158:         updatePortraitOverlay();
1159: 
1160:     } catch (Exception e) {
1161:         Log.e(TAG, "Favori değiştirilemedi", e);
1162:     }
1163: }
1164: 
1165: private void showChannelList() {
1166:     if (channels.isEmpty()) return;
1167:     if (channelDialog != null && channelDialog.isShowing()) return;
1168: 
1169:     final Dialog dialog = new Dialog(this);
1170:     channelDialog = dialog;
1171: 
1172:     final java.util.ArrayList<String> groups = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
1173:     final java.util.LinkedHashMap<String, java.util.ArrayList<Integer>> grouped =
1174:             new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>();
1175: 
1176:     for (int i = 0; i < channels.size(); i++) {
1177:         Channel c = channels.get(i);
1178:         String g = c.group == null || c.group.trim().isEmpty()
1179:                 ? "Genel" : c.group.trim();
1180: 
1181:         if (!grouped.containsKey(g)) {
1182:             grouped.put(g, new java.util.ArrayList<Integer>());
1183:             groups.add(g);
1184:         }
1185:         grouped.get(g).add(i);
1186:     }
1187: 
1188:     final int[] groupIndex = {0};
1189: 
1190:     // Açık kanalın grubunu başlangıç grubu yap.
1191:     if (selectedIndex >= 0 && selectedIndex < channels.size()) {
1192:         String selectedGroup = channels.get(selectedIndex).group;
1193:         if (selectedGroup == null || selectedGroup.trim().isEmpty()) {
1194:             selectedGroup = "Genel";
1195:         } else {
1196:             selectedGroup = selectedGroup.trim();
1197:         }
1198: 
1199:         int foundGroup = groups.indexOf(selectedGroup);
1200:         if (foundGroup >= 0) groupIndex[0] = foundGroup;
1201:     }
1202: 
1203:     final java.util.ArrayList<Integer> visibleItems = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
1204: 
1205:     LinearLayout root = new LinearLayout(this);
1206:     root.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
1207:     root.setPadding(
1208:             dp(isPortraitMode() ? 14 : 8),
1209:             dp(isPortraitMode() ? 14 : 8),
1210:             dp(isPortraitMode() ? 14 : 8),
1211:             dp(isPortraitMode() ? 12 : 8));
1212:     root.setBackgroundColor(Color.rgb(17, 21, 29));
1213: 
1214:     TextView title = new TextView(this);
1215:     title.setText("İSA TV HD  •  KANALLAR");
1216:     title.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1217:     title.setTextSize(18);
1218:     title.setTypeface(null, android.graphics.Typeface.BOLD);
1219:     title.setPadding(dp(8), dp(8), dp(8), dp(14));
1220:     root.addView(title);
1221: 
1222:     LinearLayout groupBar = new LinearLayout(this);
1223:     groupBar.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
1224:     groupBar.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
1225:     groupBar.setPadding(dp(4), dp(8), dp(4), dp(8));
1226:     groupBar.setBackgroundColor(0xFF252E3C);
1227: 
1228:     TextView previous = new TextView(this);
1229:     previous.setText("‹");
1230:     previous.setTextSize(30);
1231:     previous.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1232:     previous.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
1233:     previous.setMinWidth(dp(48));
1234: 
1235:     TextView groupName = new TextView(this);
1236:     groupName.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1237:     groupName.setTextSize(14);
1238:     groupName.setTypeface(null, android.graphics.Typeface.BOLD);
1239:     groupName.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
1240:     groupName.setMaxLines(2);
1241: 
1242:     TextView next = new TextView(this);
1243:     next.setText("›");
1244:     next.setTextSize(30);
1245:     next.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1246:     next.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
1247:     next.setMinWidth(dp(48));
1248: 
1249:     groupBar.addView(previous);
1250:     groupBar.addView(groupName, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1251:             0, dp(48), 1f));
1252:     groupBar.addView(next);
1253:     root.addView(groupBar);
1254: 
1255:     EditText search = new EditText(this);
1256:     search.setSingleLine(true);
1257:     search.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1258:     search.setHintTextColor(0xFFAAAAAA);
1259:     search.setHint("Bu grupta kanal ara...");
1260:     search.setTextSize(14);
1261:     search.setPadding(dp(12), 0, dp(12), 0);
1262:     search.setBackgroundColor(0xFF252E3C);
1263: 
1264:     LinearLayout.LayoutParams searchParams =
1265:             new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(-1, dp(isPortraitMode() ? 46 : 36));
1266:     searchParams.topMargin = dp(10);
1267:     searchParams.bottomMargin = dp(8);
1268:     root.addView(search, searchParams);
1269: 
1270:     ListView list = new ListView(this);
1271:     list.setDivider(new android.graphics.drawable.ColorDrawable(0x22FFFFFF));
1272:     list.setDividerHeight(dp(1));
1273:     list.setCacheColorHint(Color.TRANSPARENT);
1274: 
1275:     TextView count = new TextView(this);
1276:     count.setTextColor(0xFFAAAAAA);
1277:     count.setTextSize(12);
1278:     count.setPadding(dp(8), dp(8), dp(8), dp(8));
1279:     root.addView(count);
1280: 
1281:     BaseAdapter adapter = new BaseAdapter() {
1282:         @Override public int getCount() {
1283:             return visibleItems.size();
1284:         }
1285: 
1286:         @Override public Object getItem(int position) {
1287:             return channels.get(visibleItems.get(position));
1288:         }
1289: 
1290:         @Override public long getItemId(int position) {
1291:             return visibleItems.get(position);
1292:         }
1293: 
1294:         @Override public View getView(int position, View recycled,
1295:                                       android.view.ViewGroup parent) {
1296:             LinearLayout row;
1297:             ImageView icon;
1298:             LinearLayout texts;
1299:             TextView name;
1300:             TextView subtitle;
1301: 
1302:             if (recycled instanceof LinearLayout) {
1303:                 row = (LinearLayout) recycled;
1304:                 icon = (ImageView) row.getChildAt(0);
1305:                 texts = (LinearLayout) row.getChildAt(1);
1306:                 name = (TextView) texts.getChildAt(0);
1307:                 subtitle = (TextView) texts.getChildAt(1);
1308:             } else {
1309:                 row = new LinearLayout(PlayerActivity.this);
1310:                 row.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
1311:                 row.setOrientation(LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL);
1312:                 row.setPadding(dp(10), dp(8), dp(10), dp(8));
1313:                 row.setMinimumHeight(dp(isPortraitMode() ? 60 : 32));
1314: 
1315:                 icon = new ImageView(PlayerActivity.this);
1316:                 icon.setScaleType(ImageView.ScaleType.FIT_CENTER);
1317:                 row.addView(icon, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1318:                         dp(isPortraitMode() ? 46 : 26),
1319:                         dp(isPortraitMode() ? 46 : 26)));
1320: 
1321:                 texts = new LinearLayout(PlayerActivity.this);
1322:                 texts.setOrientation(LinearLayout.VERTICAL);
1323:                 texts.setPadding(dp(12), 0, 0, 0);
1324: 
1325:                 name = new TextView(PlayerActivity.this);
1326:                 name.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1327:                 name.setTextSize(isPortraitMode() ? 14 : 12);
1328:                 name.setMaxLines(1);
1329:                 name.setEllipsize(android.text.TextUtils.TruncateAt.END);
1330: 
1331:                 subtitle = new TextView(PlayerActivity.this);
1332:                 subtitle.setTextColor(0xFFAAAAAA);
1333:                 subtitle.setTextSize(isPortraitMode() ? 11 : 9);
1334: 
1335:                 texts.addView(name);
1336:                 texts.addView(subtitle);
1337:                 row.addView(texts, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(
1338:                         0, -2, 1f));
1339:             }
1340: 
1341:             int actual = visibleItems.get(position);
1342:             Channel c = channels.get(actual);
1343: 
1344:             name.setText((actual == selectedIndex ? "●  " : "")
1345:                     + (c.name == null ? "İsimsiz kanal" : c.name));
1346:             subtitle.setText(actual == selectedIndex ? "ŞU ANDA OYNUYOR" : "CANLI YAYIN");
1347:             row.setBackgroundColor(actual == selectedIndex
1348:                     ? 0xFF303D50 : Color.TRANSPARENT);
1349: 
1350:             icon.setImageDrawable(null);
1351:             if (c.logo != null && !c.logo.isEmpty()) {
1352:                 try {
1353:                     new android.os.AsyncTask<Void, Void, android.graphics.Bitmap>() {
1354:                         @Override
1355:                         protected android.graphics.Bitmap doInBackground(Void... v) {
1356:                             try {
1357:                                 return android.graphics.BitmapFactory.decodeStream(
1358:                                         new java.net.URL(c.logo).openStream());
1359:                             } catch (Exception e) {
1360:                                 return null;
1361:                             }
1362:                         }
1363:                         @Override
1364:                         protected void onPostExecute(android.graphics.Bitmap bitmap) {
1365:                             if (bitmap != null && icon.getParent() != null) {
1366:                                 icon.setImageBitmap(bitmap);
1367:                             }
1368:                         }
1369:                     }.execute();
1370:                 } catch (Exception ignored) {}
1371:             }
1372:             return row;
1373:         }
1374:     };
1375: 
1376:     Runnable updateList = () -> {
1377:         String group = groups.get(groupIndex[0]);
1378:         java.util.ArrayList<Integer> source = grouped.get(group);
1379:         visibleItems.clear();
1380: 
1381:         String term = search.getText().toString().toLowerCase(
1382:                 java.util.Locale.forLanguageTag("tr"));
1383: 
1384:         for (Integer index : source) {
1385:             String name = channels.get(index).name;
1386:             if (name == null) name = "";
1387:             if (name.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.forLanguageTag("tr"))
1388:                     .contains(term)) {
1389:                 visibleItems.add(index);
1390:             }
1391:         }
1392: 
1393:         groupName.setText(group + "\n" + source.size() + " KANAL");
1394:         count.setText(visibleItems.size() + " kanal gösteriliyor");
1395:         adapter.notifyDataSetChanged();
1396:     };
1397: 
1398:     previous.setOnClickListener(v -> {
1399:         groupIndex[0] = (groupIndex[0] - 1 + groups.size()) % groups.size();
1400:         search.setText("");
1401:         updateList.run();
1402:     });
1403: 
1404:     next.setOnClickListener(v -> {
1405:         groupIndex[0] = (groupIndex[0] + 1) % groups.size();
1406:         search.setText("");
1407:         updateList.run();
1408:     });
1409: 
1410:     search.addTextChangedListener(new android.text.TextWatcher() {
1411:         @Override public void beforeTextChanged(CharSequence s, int st, int c, int a) {}
1412:         @Override public void onTextChanged(CharSequence s, int st, int before, int count) {
1413:             updateList.run();
1414:         }
1415:         @Override public void afterTextChanged(android.text.Editable e) {}
1416:     });
1417: 
1418:     list.setAdapter(adapter);
1419:     list.setOnItemClickListener((parent, view, position, id) -> {
1420:         if (position < 0 || position >= visibleItems.size()) return;
1421:         selectedIndex = visibleItems.get(position);
1422:         dialog.dismiss();
1423:         playSelected();
1424:     });
1425: 
1426:     root.addView(list, new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(-1, 0, 1f));
1427: 
1428:     TextView close = new TextView(this);
1429:     close.setText("KAPAT");
1430:     close.setTextColor(Color.WHITE);
1431:     close.setGravity(Gravity.CENTER);
1432:     close.setTextSize(13);
1433:     close.setBackgroundColor(0xFF303D50);
1434:     close.setPadding(0, dp(12), 0, dp(12));
1435:     close.setOnClickListener(v -> dialog.dismiss());
1436: 
1437:     LinearLayout.LayoutParams closeParams =
1438:             new LinearLayout.LayoutParams(-1, dp(isPortraitMode() ? 46 : 34));
1439:     closeParams.topMargin = dp(4);
1440:     root.addView(close, closeParams);
1441: 
1442:     updateList.run();
1443: 
1444:     int selectedPosition = visibleItems.indexOf(selectedIndex);
1445:     if (selectedPosition >= 0) {
1446:         list.setSelection(selectedPosition);
1447:     }
1448: 
1449:     dialog.setContentView(root);
1450:     dialog.setCancelable(true);
1451:     dialog.setOnDismissListener(d -> {
1452:         if (channelDialog == dialog) channelDialog = null;
1453:     });
1454:     dialog.show();
1455: 
1456:     if (dialog.getWindow() != null) {
1457:         dialog.getWindow().setBackgroundDrawableResource(
1458:                 android.R.color.transparent);
1459:         int screenWidth = getResources().getDisplayMetrics().widthPixels;
1460:         int screenHeight = getResources().getDisplayMetrics().heightPixels;
1461:         int panelWidth = isPortraitMode()
1462:                 ? (int)(screenWidth * 0.94f)
1463:                 : Math.min(dp(430), (int)(screenWidth * 0.42f));
1464: 
1465:         dialog.getWindow().setLayout(
1466:                 panelWidth,
1467:                 (int)(screenHeight * 0.96f));
1468:         dialog.getWindow().setGravity(
1469:                 Gravity.LEFT | Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL);
1470:         dialog.getWindow().addFlags(WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_DIM_BEHIND);
1471:         WindowManager.LayoutParams params = dialog.getWindow().getAttributes();
1472:         params.dimAmount = 0.72f;
1473:         dialog.getWindow().setAttributes(params);
1474:     }
1475: }

1479:     private boolean shouldShowTvLogo() {
1480:         return true;
1481:     }
1482: 
1483:     private int dp(float value) {
1484:         return (int) (
1485:                 value * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density
1486:                 + 0.5f
1487:         );
1488:     }
1489: 
1490:     @Override
1491:     public void onBackPressed() {
1492:         super.onBackPressed();
1493:     }
1494: 
1495:     
1496: 
1497:     
1498: 
1499:     
1500: 
1501:     @Override
1502:     protected void onSaveInstanceState(Bundle outState) {
1503:         outState.putInt("isa_saved_channel_index", selectedIndex);
1504:         super.onSaveInstanceState(outState);
1505:     }
1506: 
1507:     @Override
1508:     public void onConfigurationChanged(Configuration newConfig) {
1509:         super.onConfigurationChanged(newConfig);
1510: 
1511:         // Mevcut ExoPlayer ve video yüzeyini değiştirme.
1512:         // Böylece yayın bağlantısı yeniden kurulmaz.
1513:         if (channelDialog != null && channelDialog.isShowing()) {
1514:             channelDialog.dismiss();
1515:         }
1516: 
1517:         if (logo != null &&
1518:                 logo.getLayoutParams() instanceof FrameLayout.LayoutParams) {
1519:             boolean portrait = isPortraitMode();
1520: 
1521:             FrameLayout.LayoutParams lp =
1522:                     (FrameLayout.LayoutParams) logo.getLayoutParams();
1523: 
1524:             lp.width = dp(portrait ? 68 : 130);
1525:             lp.height = dp(portrait ? 42 : 80);
1526:             lp.gravity = Gravity.TOP | Gravity.RIGHT;
1527:             lp.setMargins(
1528:                     0,
1529:                     dp(portrait ? 8 : 12),
1530:                     dp(portrait ? 8 : 12),
1531:                     0);
1532: 
1533:             logo.setLayoutParams(lp);
1534:             logo.setPadding(
1535:                     portrait ? 0 : dp(5),
1536:                     portrait ? 0 : dp(5),
1537:                     portrait ? 0 : dp(5),
1538:                     portrait ? 0 : dp(5));
1539:             logo.setBackgroundColor(
1540:                     portrait ? Color.TRANSPARENT : 0x880F0F0F);
1541:         }
1542: 
1543:         updatePortraitOverlay();
1544: 
1545:         applyPreloadPolicy();
1546: 
1547:         if (channelButton != null) {
1548:             channelButton.setVisibility(View.GONE);
1549:         }
1550:         applyImmersiveMode();
1551:     }
1552: 
1553:     @Override
1554:     protected void onPause() {
1555:         super.onPause();
1556: 
1557:         handler.removeCallbacks(neighborPreloadTimeout);
1558:         stopNeighborPreloads();
1559: 
1560:         if (player != null) player.pause();
1561:     }
1562: 
1563:     @Override
1564:     protected void onResume() {
1565:         super.onResume();
1566: 
1567:         if (player != null) {
1568:             applyPreloadPolicy();
1569:         }
1570:     }
1571: 
1572:     @Override
1573:     protected void onDestroy() {
1574:         handler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
1575: 
1576:         if (playerView != null) {
1577:             playerView.setPlayer(null);
1578:         }
1579: 
1580:         java.util.HashSet<ExoPlayer> allPlayers =
1581:                 new java.util.HashSet<>();
1582: 
1583:         if (player != null) allPlayers.add(player);
1584:         allPlayers.addAll(preloadedPlayers.values());
1585:         allPlayers.addAll(standbyPlayers);
1586: 
1587:         preloadedPlayers.clear();
1588:         standbyPlayers.clear();
1589: 
1590:         for (ExoPlayer p : allPlayers) {
1591:             try {
1592:                 p.release();
1593:             } catch (Exception ignored) {}
1594:         }
1595: 
1596:         player = null;
1597: 
1598:         super.onDestroy();
1599:     }
1600: 
1601: }

114:     private void loadQueue(String fallbackUrl) {
115:         String path = getIntent().getStringExtra("queue_file");
116:         boolean loaded = false;
117: 
118:         if (path != null) {
119:             try (java.io.FileInputStream input =
120:                          new java.io.FileInputStream(path);
121:                  java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream bytes =
122:                          new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()) {
123: 
124:                 byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
125:                 int count;
126:                 while ((count = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
127:                     bytes.write(buffer, 0, count);
128:                 }
129: 
130:                 JSONArray array = new JSONArray(bytes.toString("UTF-8"));
131:                 for (int i = 0; i < array.length(); i++) {
132:                     JSONObject item = array.optJSONObject(i);
133:                     if (item == null) continue;
134: 
135:                     String itemUrl = item.optString("url", "");
136:                     if (itemUrl.matches("(?i)^https?://.*")) {
137:                         queue.add(item);
138:                     }
139:                 }
140:                 loaded = !queue.isEmpty();
141: 
142:             } catch (Exception e) {
143:                 android.util.Log.e("ISAFLIX", "Kuyruk okunamadı", e);
144:             }
145:         }
146: 
147:         if (!loaded) {
148:             JSONObject one = new JSONObject();
149:             try {
150:                 one.put("name", getIntent().getStringExtra("channel_name"));
151:                 one.put("url", fallbackUrl);
152:                 one.put("headers", new JSONObject(
153:                         getIntent().getStringExtra("headers_json") == null
154:                                 ? "{}"
155:                                 : getIntent().getStringExtra("headers_json")
156:                 ));
157:                 one.put("is_live", false);
158:             } catch (Exception ignored) {}
159:             queue.add(one);
160:         }
161:     }

304:     private boolean isDirectMediaUrl(String value) {
305:         if (value == null) return false;
306:         String lower = value.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
307:         try {
308:             String path = Uri.parse(value).getPath();
309:             if (path != null && path.matches(
310:                     "(?i).*\\.(m3u8|mpd|mp4|m4v|webm|mkv|mov|3gp)$")) {
311:                 return true;
312:             }
313:         } catch (Exception ignored) {}
314:         return lower.contains("format=m3u8")
315:                 || lower.contains("type=hls")
316:                 || lower.contains("format=mpd")
317:                 || lower.contains("type=dash");
318:     }

321:     private void startWebsitePlayback(String pageUrl) {
322:         websitePlaybackMode = true;
323:         websiteMediaAccepted = false;
324:         resolvingCancelled = false;
325:         websitePageUrl = pageUrl;
326:         websiteMediaCandidates.clear();
327:         websiteHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
328: 
329:         int selected = getIntent().getIntExtra("selected_index", 0);
330:         if (selected < 0 || selected >= queue.size()) selected = 0;
331:         JSONObject chosen = queue.isEmpty() ? new JSONObject() : queue.get(selected);
332:         queue.clear();
333:         queue.add(chosen);
334: 
335:         playerView.setVisibility(View.GONE);
336:         loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
337:         loadingLabel.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
338:         status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
339: 
340:         webView = new WebView(this);
341:         webView.setVisibility(View.INVISIBLE);
342:         webView.setBackgroundColor(Color.BLACK);
343:         webView.setImportantForAccessibility(View.IMPORTANT_FOR_ACCESSIBILITY_NO);
344: 
345:         WebSettings settings = webView.getSettings();
346:         settings.setJavaScriptEnabled(true);
347:         settings.setDomStorageEnabled(true);
348:         settings.setDatabaseEnabled(true);
349:         settings.setMediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture(false);
350:         settings.setSupportMultipleWindows(false);
351:         settings.setJavaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically(false);
352:         settings.setAllowFileAccess(false);
353:         settings.setAllowContentAccess(false);
354:         settings.setMixedContentMode(WebSettings.MIXED_CONTENT_NEVER_ALLOW);
355: 
356:         CookieManager cookies = CookieManager.getInstance();
357:         cookies.setAcceptCookie(true);
358:         cookies.setAcceptThirdPartyCookies(webView, true);
359:         webView.addJavascriptInterface(new WebsiteBridge(), "ISAWebsiteBridge");
360: 
361:         final String initialHost;
362:         try {
363:             initialHost = Uri.parse(pageUrl).getHost();
364:             if (initialHost == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Host yok");
365:         } catch (Exception e) {
366:             status.setText("İçerik adresi okunamadı.");
367:             status.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
368:             return;
369:         }
370: 
371:         webView.setWebChromeClient(new WebChromeClient() {
372:             @Override
373:             public boolean onCreateWindow(WebView view, boolean dialog,
374:                     boolean userGesture, android.os.Message resultMsg) {
375:                 return false;
376:             }
377:         });
378: 
379:         webView.setWebViewClient(new WebViewClient() {
380:             @Override
381:             public boolean shouldOverrideUrlLoading(WebView view, String url) {
382:                 try {
383:                     Uri target = Uri.parse(url);
384:                     String scheme = target.getScheme();
385:                     if (!"http".equalsIgnoreCase(scheme)
386:                             && !"https".equalsIgnoreCase(scheme)) return true;
387:                     String host = target.getHost();
388:                     return host == null || !host.equalsIgnoreCase(initialHost);
389:                 } catch (Exception e) {
390:                     return true;
391:                 }
392:             }
393: 
394:             @Override
395:             public boolean shouldOverrideUrlLoading(WebView view,
396:                     android.webkit.WebResourceRequest request) {
397:                 if (request == null || request.getUrl() == null) return true;
398: 
399:                 // Video oynatıcı iframe'leri farklı alan adlarında olabilir.
400:                 // Alt çerçevelerin yüklenmesine izin ver; ana sayfa dışına
401:                 // yapılan reklam/yönlendirme geçişlerini engelle.
402:                 if (!request.isForMainFrame()) return false;
403: 
404:                 String targetHost = request.getUrl().getHost();
405:                 String scheme = request.getUrl().getScheme();
406: 
407:                 if (!"http".equalsIgnoreCase(scheme)
408:                         && !"https".equalsIgnoreCase(scheme)) return true;
409: 
410:                 return targetHost == null
411:                         || !targetHost.equalsIgnoreCase(initialHost);
412:             }
413: 
414:             @Override
415:             public android.webkit.WebResourceResponse shouldInterceptRequest(
416:                     WebView view, android.webkit.WebResourceRequest request) {
417:                 if (request != null && request.getUrl() != null)
418:                     collectWebsiteMedia(request.getUrl().toString());
419:                 return super.shouldInterceptRequest(view, request);
420:             }
421: 
422:             @Override
423:             public android.webkit.WebResourceResponse shouldInterceptRequest(
424:                     WebView view, String url) {
425:                 collectWebsiteMedia(url);
426:                 return super.shouldInterceptRequest(view, url);
427:             }
428: 
429:             @Override
430:             public void onPageFinished(WebView view, String url) {
431:                 super.onPageFinished(view, url);
432:                 installWebsiteDetector();
433:             }
434:         });
435: 
436:         root.addView(webView, 0, new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
437:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
438:                 FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT));
439:         webView.loadUrl(pageUrl);
440: 
441:         websiteHandler.postDelayed(() -> {
442:             if (websitePlaybackMode && !websiteMediaAccepted) {
443:                 loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
444:                 loadingLabel.setVisibility(View.GONE);
445:                 status.setText("Video kaynağı bulunamadı. Site medya adresi sağlamadı.");
446:                 status.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
447:             }
448:         }, 30000L);
449:     }

452:     private boolean isAudioOnlyUrl(String url) {
453:         if (url == null) return false;
454:         String u = url.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
455:         return u.contains("/mp4a/") || u.contains("/audio/")
456:                 || u.matches("(?s).*\\.(aac|mp3|m4a|opus)(?:[?#].*)?$");
457:     }
458: 
459:     private void collectWebsiteMedia(String url) {
460:         if (resolvingCancelled || websiteMediaAccepted || !websitePlaybackMode) return;
461:         if (url == null || !url.matches("(?i)^https?://.*")) return;
462:         if (!isDirectMediaUrl(url)) return;
463: 
464:         synchronized (websiteMediaCandidates) {
465:             websiteMediaCandidates.add(url);
466:         }
467:         android.util.Log.d("ISAFLIX", "Medya bulundu: " + url);
468:         websiteHandler.removeCallbacks(websiteResolveRunnable);
469:         websiteHandler.postDelayed(websiteResolveRunnable, 6000L);
470:     }
471: 
472:     private void installWebsiteDetector() {
473:         if (webView == null) return;
474:         String script =
475:             "(function(){" +
476:             "if(window.__isaDetectorInstalled)return;" +
477:             "window.__isaDetectorInstalled=true;" +
478:             "function send(u){try{if(u&&/^https?:\\/\\//i.test(u))" +
479:             "ISAWebsiteBridge.onResource(u);}catch(e){}}" +
480:             "function scanDoc(d,depth){try{" +
481:             "if(!d||depth>4)return;" +
482:             "var a=d.querySelectorAll('video,audio,source');" +
483:             "for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){var x=a[i];" +
484:             "send(x.currentSrc||x.src||x.getAttribute('src'));" +
485:             "send(x.getAttribute('data-src'));" +
486:             "send(x.getAttribute('data-url'));}" +
487:             "var f=d.querySelectorAll('iframe,frame');" +
488:             "for(var k=0;k<f.length;k++){" +
489:             "var z=f[k];send(z.src||z.getAttribute('src'));" +
490:             "send(z.getAttribute('data-src'));" +
491:             "try{if(z.contentDocument)scanDoc(z.contentDocument,depth+1);}catch(e){}}" +
492:             "var r=d.defaultView.performance.getEntriesByType('resource');" +
493:             "for(var j=0;j<r.length;j++)send(r[j].name);" +
494:             "}catch(e){}}" +
495:             "function scan(){scanDoc(document,0);}" +
496:             "document.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',scan,true);" +
497:             "document.addEventListener('canplay',scan,true);" +
498:             "document.addEventListener('load',scan,true);" +
499:             "try{new MutationObserver(scan).observe(document.documentElement||document," +
500:             "{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true," +
501:             "attributeFilter:['src','data-src','data-url']});}catch(e){}" +
502:             "setInterval(scan,500);scan();})();";
503:         webView.evaluateJavascript(script, null);
504:     }
505: 
506:     private class WebsiteBridge {
507:         @JavascriptInterface
508:         public void onResource(String url) {
509:             collectWebsiteMedia(url);
510:         }
511:     }
512: 
513:     private void finishWebsiteDetection() {
514:         if (resolvingCancelled || websiteMediaAccepted || !websitePlaybackMode) return;
515: 
516:         String video = null;
517:         String audio = null;
518:         synchronized (websiteMediaCandidates) {
519:             for (String url : websiteMediaCandidates) {
520:                 String u = url.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
521:                 if (u.contains("/mp4a/")) audio = url;
522:                 else if (u.contains("/avc1/")) video = url;
523:             }
524:             if (video == null) {
525:                 for (String url : websiteMediaCandidates) {
526:                     if (!isAudioOnlyUrl(url)) {
527:                         video = url;
528:                         break;
529:                     }
530:                 }
531:             }
532:         }
533:         if (video != null) acceptWebsiteMedia(video, audio);
534:     }
535: 
536:     private void acceptWebsiteMedia(String mediaUrl, String audioUrl) {
537:         if (resolvingCancelled || websiteMediaAccepted || !websitePlaybackMode) return;
538:         if (mediaUrl == null || !isDirectMediaUrl(mediaUrl)) return;
539: 
540:         websiteMediaAccepted = true;
541:         try {
542:             JSONObject item = queue.get(0);
543:             item.put("url", mediaUrl);
544:             if (audioUrl != null) item.put("audio_url", audioUrl);
545: 
546:             JSONObject headers = item.optJSONObject("headers");
547:             if (headers == null) headers = new JSONObject();
548:             headers.put("Referer", websitePageUrl);
549:             String cookie = CookieManager.getInstance().getCookie(websitePageUrl);
550:             if (cookie != null && !cookie.isEmpty()) headers.put("Cookie", cookie);
551:             item.put("headers", headers);
552: 
553:             websiteHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
554:             if (webView != null) {
555:                 webView.stopLoading();
556:                 webView.loadUrl("about:blank");
557:                 root.removeView(webView);
558:                 webView.destroy();
559:                 webView = null;
560:             }
561:             websitePlaybackMode = false;
562:             loadingLabel.setVisibility(View.GONE);
563:             playerView.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
564:             loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
565:             initializePlayer();
566:         } catch (Exception e) {
567:             websiteMediaAccepted = false;
568:             android.util.Log.e("ISAFLIX", "Medya aktarımı başarısız", e);
569:         }
570:     }
571: 
572:     private MediaSource createMediaSource(JSONObject entry) {
573:         String url = entry.optString("url", "");
574:         JSONObject jsonHeaders = entry.optJSONObject("headers");
575: 
576:         Map<String, String> headers = new HashMap<>();
577:         String userAgent =
578:                 "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 11) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/120 Safari/537.36";
579: 
580:         if (jsonHeaders != null) {
581:             Iterator<String> keys = jsonHeaders.keys();
582:             while (keys.hasNext()) {
583:                 String key = keys.next();
584:                 String value = jsonHeaders.optString(key, "");
585: 
586:                 if (!key.matches("(?i)[a-z0-9-]+")
587:                         || key.matches("(?i)host|content-length|connection|transfer-encoding|accept-encoding")
588:                         || value.isEmpty()
589:                         || value.contains("\r")
590:                         || value.contains("\n")) {
591:                     continue;
592:                 }
593: 
594:                 if (key.equalsIgnoreCase("User-Agent")) {
595:                     userAgent = value;
596:                 } else {
597:                     headers.put(key, value);
598:                 }
599:             }
600:         }
601: 
602:         DefaultHttpDataSource.Factory factory =
603:                 new DefaultHttpDataSource.Factory()
604:                         .setUserAgent(userAgent)
605:                         .setConnectTimeoutMs(20000)
606:                         .setReadTimeoutMs(60000)
607:                         .setAllowCrossProtocolRedirects(true)
608:                         .setDefaultRequestProperties(headers);
609: 
610:         MediaItem mediaItem = new MediaItem.Builder()
611:                 .setUri(Uri.parse(url))
612:                 .setMediaId(entry.optString("name", url))
613:                 .build();
614: 
615:         String path = Uri.parse(url).getPath();
616:         path = path == null ? "" : path.toLowerCase();
617:         String lowerUrl = url.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
618:         boolean isHls = path.endsWith(".m3u8")
619:                 || lowerUrl.matches("(?s).*\\.m3u8(?:[?#].*)?$")
620:                 || lowerUrl.contains("format=m3u8")
621:                 || lowerUrl.contains("type=hls");
622:         boolean isDash = path.endsWith(".mpd")
623:                 || lowerUrl.matches("(?s).*\\.mpd(?:[?#].*)?$")
624:                 || lowerUrl.contains("format=mpd")
625:                 || lowerUrl.contains("type=dash");
626: 
627:         if (isHls) {
628:             return new HlsMediaSource.Factory(factory)
629:                     .setAllowChunklessPreparation(true)
630:                     .createMediaSource(mediaItem);
631:         }
632: 
633:         if (isDash) {
634:             return new DashMediaSource.Factory(factory)
635:                     .createMediaSource(mediaItem);
636:         }
637: 
638:         return new DefaultMediaSourceFactory(factory)
639:                 .createMediaSource(mediaItem);
640:     }
641: 
642:     private void initializePlayer() {
643:         try {
644:             ArrayList<MediaSource> sources = new ArrayList<>();
645: 
646:             for (JSONObject item : queue) {
647:                 MediaSource videoSource = createMediaSource(item);
648:                 String audioUrl = item.optString("audio_url", "");
649:                 if (!audioUrl.isEmpty()) {
650:                     JSONObject audioItem = new JSONObject(item.toString());
651:                     audioItem.put("url", audioUrl);
652:                     audioItem.remove("audio_url");
653:                     MediaSource audioSource = createMediaSource(audioItem);
654:                     sources.add(new MergingMediaSource(true, videoSource, audioSource));
655:                 } else {
656:                     sources.add(videoSource);
657:                 }
658:             }
659: 
660:             if (sources.isEmpty()) {
661:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Oynatma listesi boş.");
662:             }
663: 
664:             int index = getIntent().getIntExtra("selected_index", 0);
665:             if (index < 0 || index >= sources.size()) index = 0;
666: 
667:             player = new ExoPlayer.Builder(this).build();
668:             playerView.setPlayer(player);
669: 
670:             player.addListener(new Player.Listener() {
671:                 @Override
672:                 public void onPlaybackStateChanged(int state) {
673:                     if (state == Player.STATE_READY) {
674:                         loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
675:                         status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
676:                         playerView.showController();
677:                     } else if (state == Player.STATE_BUFFERING) {
678:                         status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
679:                         loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
680:                     } else if (state == Player.STATE_ENDED) {
681:                         loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
682:                         status.setVisibility(View.GONE);
683:                     }
684:                 }
685: 
686:                 @Override
687:                 public void onMediaItemTransition(
688:                         MediaItem mediaItem, int reason) {
689:                     updateTitle();
690:                     updateFavoriteButton();
691:                 }
692: 
693:                 @Override
694:                 public void onPlayerError(PlaybackException error) {
695:                     loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
696:                     status.setText("İçerik şu anda oynatılamıyor.");
697:                     status.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
698:                     android.util.Log.e(
699:                             "ISAFLIX", "Oynatma hatası", error
700:                     );
701:                 }
702:             });
703: 
704:             MediaSource[] array = sources.toArray(new MediaSource[0]);
705:             ConcatenatingMediaSource playlist =
706:                     new ConcatenatingMediaSource(array);
707: 
708:             player.setMediaSource(playlist);
709:             player.seekTo(index, 0L);
710:             player.prepare();
711:             player.play();
712:             updateTitle();
713:             updateFavoriteButton();
714: 
715:         } catch (Exception e) {
716:             android.util.Log.e("ISAFLIX", "Oynatıcı başlatılamadı", e);
717:             if (loadingSpinner != null) loadingSpinner.setVisibility(View.GONE);
718:             status.setText("İçerik şu anda oynatılamıyor.");
719:             status.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
720:         }
721:     }
722: 
723: 
724:     private void updateTitle() {
725:         if (player == null || queue.isEmpty()) return;
726: 
727:         int index = player.getCurrentMediaItemIndex();
728:         if (index < 0 || index >= queue.size()) index = 0;
729: 
730:         JSONObject item = queue.get(index);
731:         String name = item.optString(
732:                 "name",
733:                 getIntent().getStringExtra("channel_name")
734:         );
735: 
736:         titleView.setText(name == null ? "İSAFLIX" : name);
737: 
738:         int season = item.optInt("season", 0);
739:         int episode = item.optInt("episode", 0);
740: 
741:         infoHandler.removeCallbacks(hideEpisodeInfo);
742: 
743:         if (season > 0 || episode > 0) {
744:             episodeView.animate().cancel();
745:             episodeView.setAlpha(1f);
746: 
747:             String episodeInfo;
748:             if (season > 0 && episode > 0) {
749:                 episodeInfo = "Sezon " + season + "  ·  " + episode + ". Bölüm";
750:             } else if (season > 0) {
751:                 episodeInfo = "Sezon " + season;
752:             } else {
753:                 episodeInfo = episode + ". Bölüm";
754:             }
755: 
756:             episodeView.setText(episodeInfo);
757: 
758:             boolean landscape =
759:                     getResources().getConfiguration().orientation
760:                     == Configuration.ORIENTATION_LANDSCAPE;
761: 
762:             episodeView.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);
763: 
764:             if (landscape) {
765:                 infoHandler.postDelayed(hideEpisodeInfo, 5000L);
766:             }
767:         } else {
768:             episodeView.setVisibility(View.GONE);
769:         }
770:     }
771: 
772:     private String currentFavoriteId(JSONObject item) {
773:         boolean series = item.optBoolean("is_series", false)
774:                 || "series".equalsIgnoreCase(item.optString("type", ""))
775:                 || item.optInt("season", 0) > 0;
776: 
777:         String parentId = item.optString("series_id",
778:                 item.optString("parent_id", ""));
779:         if (series) {
780:             String parentName = item.optString("series_name",
781:                     item.optString("name", "İSAFLIX dizisi"));
782:             return "series:" + (parentId.isEmpty() ? parentName : parentId);
783:         }
784: 
785:         String id = item.optString("id", "");
786:         return "movie:" + (id.isEmpty()
787:                 ? item.optString("url", "") : id);
788:     }
789: 
790:     private void updateFavoriteButton() {
791:         if (favoriteButton == null || queue.isEmpty()) return;
792: 
793:         int index = player == null ? 0 : player.getCurrentMediaItemIndex();
794:         if (index < 0 || index >= queue.size()) index = 0;
795: 
796:         JSONObject item = queue.get(index);
797:         String favoriteId = currentFavoriteId(item);
798:         android.content.SharedPreferences prefs =
799:                 getSharedPreferences("isa_tv_data", MODE_PRIVATE);
800: 
801:         boolean saved = false;
802:         try {
803:             JSONArray favorites = new JSONArray(
804:                     prefs.getString("isaflix_favorites", "[]"));
805:             for (int i = 0; i < favorites.length(); i++) {
806:                 JSONObject favorite = favorites.optJSONObject(i);
807:                 if (favorite != null
808:                         && favoriteId.equals(favorite.optString("favorite_id", ""))) {
809:                     saved = true;
810:                     break;
811:                 }
812:             }
813:         } catch (Exception e) {
814:             android.util.Log.e("ISAFLIX", "Favoriler okunamadı", e);
815:         }
816: 
817:         favoriteButton.setText(saved ? "♥" : "♡");
818:         favoriteButton.setTextColor(saved ? 0xFFFF3B55 : Color.WHITE);
819:         favoriteButton.setContentDescription(
820:                 saved ? "Favorilerden çıkar" : "Favorilere ekle");
821:     }
822: 
823:     private void toggleCurrentFavorite() {
824:         if (queue.isEmpty()) return;
825: 
826:         int index = player == null ? 0 : player.getCurrentMediaItemIndex();
827:         if (index < 0 || index >= queue.size()) index = 0;
828: 
829:         JSONObject item = queue.get(index);
830:         String favoriteId = currentFavoriteId(item);
831:         boolean series = favoriteId.startsWith("series:");
832: 
833:         android.content.SharedPreferences prefs =
834:                 getSharedPreferences("isa_tv_data", MODE_PRIVATE);
835: 
836:         try {
837:             JSONArray old = new JSONArray(
838:                     prefs.getString("isaflix_favorites", "[]"));
839:             JSONArray updated = new JSONArray();
840:             boolean existed = false;
841: 
842:             for (int i = 0; i < old.length(); i++) {
843:                 JSONObject favorite = old.optJSONObject(i);
844:                 if (favorite == null) continue;
845: 
846:                 if (favoriteId.equals(favorite.optString("favorite_id", ""))) {
847:                     existed = true;
848:                 } else {
849:                     updated.put(favorite);
850:                 }
851:             }
852: 
853:             if (!existed) {
854:                 JSONObject record = new JSONObject();
855:                 record.put("favorite_id", favoriteId);
856:                 record.put("name", series
857:                         ? item.optString("series_name",
858:                             item.optString("name", "İSAFLIX dizisi"))
859:                         : item.optString("name", "İSAFLIX filmi"));
860:                 record.put("url", series
861:                         ? item.optString("series_url",
862:                             item.optString("url", ""))
863:                         : item.optString("url", ""));
864:                 record.put("logo", item.optString("logo",
865:                         item.optString("poster", "")));
866:                 record.put("type", series ? "series" : "movie");
867:                 record.put("is_series", series);
868:                 if (series) {
869:                     record.put("series_id",
870:                             item.optString("series_id",
871:                                 item.optString("parent_id", "")));
872:                 }
873:                 updated.put(record);
874:             }
875: 
876:             prefs.edit().putString("isaflix_favorites",
877:                     updated.toString()).apply();
878:             updateFavoriteButton();
879: 
880:         } catch (Exception e) {
881:             android.util.Log.e("ISAFLIX", "Favori kaydedilemedi", e);
882:         }
883:     }
884: 
885:     private void createTvLogo() {
886:         Configuration c = getResources().getConfiguration();
887: 
888:         boolean landscape =
889:                 c.orientation == Configuration.ORIENTATION_LANDSCAPE;
890:         boolean television =
891:                 (c.uiMode & Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_MASK)
892:                         == Configuration.UI_MODE_TYPE_TELEVISION;
893:         boolean tablet = c.smallestScreenWidthDp >= 600;
894: 
895:         if (!landscape || (!television && !tablet)) return;
896: 
897:         try {
898:             logo = new ImageView(this);
899: 
900:             try (java.io.InputStream input =
901:                          getAssets().open("isa-tv-hd/assets/logo.png")) {
902:                 logo.setImageBitmap(BitmapFactory.decodeStream(input));
903:             }
904: 
905:             logo.setScaleType(ImageView.ScaleType.FIT_CENTER);
906:             logo.setPadding(dp(5), dp(5), dp(5), dp(5));
907:             logo.setBackgroundColor(0x880F0F0F);
908:             logo.setContentDescription("İSA TV HD");
909: 
910:             FrameLayout.LayoutParams params =
911:                     new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
912:                             dp(145),
913:                             dp(90),
914:                             Gravity.TOP | Gravity.RIGHT
915:                     );
916:             params.setMargins(0, dp(16), dp(22), 0);
917:             root.addView(logo, params);
918: 
919:         } catch (Exception e) {
920:             android.util.Log.e("ISAFLIX", "Logo yüklenemedi", e);
921:         }
922:     }
923: 
924:     @Override
925:     public boolean dispatchKeyEvent(KeyEvent event) {
926:         if (event.getAction() == KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN
927:                 && event.getRepeatCount() == 0
928:                 && player != null) {
929: 
930:             int key = event.getKeyCode();
931: 
932:             if (key == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_UP
933:                     || key == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_CHANNEL_UP
934:                     || key == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_PAGE_UP) {
935:                 if (player.hasNextMediaItem()) {
936:                     player.seekToNextMediaItem();
937:                 }
938:                 return true;
939:             }
940: 
941:             if (key == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DPAD_DOWN
942:                     || key == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_CHANNEL_DOWN
943:                     || key == KeyEvent.KEYCODE_PAGE_DOWN) {
944:                 if (player.hasPreviousMediaItem()) {
945:                     player.seekToPreviousMediaItem();
946:                 }
947:                 return true;
948:             }
949:         }
950: 
951:         return super.dispatchKeyEvent(event);
952:     }
953: 
954:     private int dp(float value) {
955:         return (int)(
956:                 value * getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density + 0.5f
957:         );
958:     }
959: 
960:     private void applyImmersiveMode() {
961:         getWindow().getDecorView().setSystemUiVisibility(
962:                 View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_IMMERSIVE_STICKY
963:                 | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_FULLSCREEN
964:                 | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_HIDE_NAVIGATION
965:                 | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_FULLSCREEN
966:                 | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_HIDE_NAVIGATION
967:                 | View.SYSTEM_UI_FLAG_LAYOUT_STABLE
968:         );
969:     }
970: 
971:     @Override
972:     public void onWindowFocusChanged(boolean hasFocus) {
973:         super.onWindowFocusChanged(hasFocus);
974:         if (hasFocus) applyImmersiveMode();
975:     }
976: 
977:     @Override
978:     public void onBackPressed() {
979:         resolvingCancelled = true;
980:         websitePlaybackMode = false;
981:         websiteMediaAccepted = true;
982:         if (webView != null) {
983:             webView.stopLoading();
984:             webView.onPause();
985:         }
986:         finish();
987:     }
988: 
989:     @Override
990:     protected void onPause() {
991:         if (player != null) player.pause();
992:         if (webView != null) webView.onPause();
993:         super.onPause();
994:     }
995: 
996:     @Override
997:     protected void onDestroy() {
998:         infoHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
999:         websiteHandler.removeCallbacksAndMessages(null);
1000:         if (playerView != null) playerView.setPlayer(null);
1001:         if (player != null) {
1002:             player.release();
1003:             player = null;
1004:         }
1005:         resolvingCancelled = true;
1006:         websitePlaybackMode = false;
1007:         if (webView != null) {
1008:             webView.stopLoading();
1009:             webView.destroy();
1010:             webView = null;
1011:         }
1012:         super.onDestroy();
1013:     }
1014: }

1: package com.isatvhd.player;
2: 
3: import android.app.Activity;
4: import android.os.Bundle;
5: import android.os.Handler;
6: import android.os.Looper;
7: import android.animation.ValueAnimator;
8: import android.graphics.Color;
9: import android.view.Gravity;
10: import android.widget.FrameLayout;
11: import android.widget.ImageView;
12: import android.content.Intent;
13: 
14: public class SplashActivity extends Activity {
15: 
16:     @Override
17:     protected void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
18:         super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);
19: 
20:         getWindow().setBackgroundDrawableResource(android.R.color.transparent);
21:         getWindow().setStatusBarColor(Color.TRANSPARENT);
22:         getWindow().setNavigationBarColor(Color.TRANSPARENT);
23: 
24:         FrameLayout splash = new FrameLayout(this);
25:         splash.setBackgroundColor(Color.TRANSPARENT);
26: 
27:         ImageView logo = new ImageView(this);
28:         logo.setImageResource(R.drawable.logo);
29:         logo.setScaleType(ImageView.ScaleType.FIT_CENTER);
30:         logo.setAlpha(0.05f);
31:         logo.setScaleX(0.86f);
32:         logo.setScaleY(0.86f);
33: 
34:         float density = getResources().getDisplayMetrics().density;
35:         logo.setTranslationX(18 * density);
36: 
37:         FrameLayout.LayoutParams params = new FrameLayout.LayoutParams(
38:                 (int)(300 * density),
39:                 (int)(200 * density),
40:                 Gravity.CENTER
41:         );
42: 
43:         splash.addView(logo, params);
44:         setContentView(splash);
45: 
46:         ValueAnimator animator = ValueAnimator.ofFloat(0f, 1f);
47:         animator.setDuration(6000);
48: 
49:         animator.addUpdateListener(animation -> {
50:             float progress = (float) animation.getAnimatedValue();
51: 
52:             // İlk 3 saniyede tamamen görünür ve aydınlık.
53:             float alpha = 0.05f + Math.min(1f, progress * 2f) * 0.95f;
54:             logo.setAlpha(alpha);
55: 
56:             // 6 saniye boyunca kesintisiz yakınlaşma.
57:             float scale = 0.86f + progress * 0.34f;
58:             logo.setScaleX(scale);
59:             logo.setScaleY(scale);
60:         });
61: 
62:         animator.start();
63: 
64:         new Handler(Looper.getMainLooper()).postDelayed(() -> {
65:             startActivity(new Intent(SplashActivity.this, MainActivity.class));
66:             overridePendingTransition(0, 0);
67:             finish();
68:         }, 6000);
69:     }
70: }

12:         this.allChannelsChunkSize = 50;

14:         this.channelStatusTtlMs = 10 * 60 * 1000;
15:         this.channelStatusCache = this.loadChannelStatusCache();
16:         this.statusProbeQueue = [];
17:         this.statusProbeInFlight = new Set();
18:         this.statusProbeWorkers = 0;

34:     detectAppMode() {
35:         const fromDesktopBridge = (window.streamflixDesktop && window.streamflixDesktop.appMode) || '';
36:         const fromQuery = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('mode') || '';
37:         const normalized = (fromDesktopBridge || fromQuery || 'web').toLowerCase();
38:         return normalized === 'desktop' ? 'desktop' : 'web';
39:     }
40: 
41:     applyAppMode() {
42:         document.body.dataset.appMode = this.appMode;
43:         if (!this.isDesktopApp) {
44:             return;
45:         }
46:         document.querySelectorAll('[data-web-only]').forEach((element) => element.remove());
47:     }
48: 
49:     initializeElements() {
50:         this.watchNowBtn = document.getElementById('watch-now');

74:         }

151:     getChannelCacheKey() {
152:         return `streamflix-channels-${this.selectedPlaylist}`;
153:     }
154: 
155:     setPreferredPlaylist(type) {
156:         if (!this.playlists[type]) return;
157:         this.selectedPlaylist = type;
158:         localStorage.setItem('streamflix-preferred-playlist', type);
159:     }
160: 
161:     async loadChannels(options = {}) {
162:         const { forceRefresh = false } = options;
163:         try {
164:             // Load from localStorage first
165:             const cacheKey = this.getChannelCacheKey();
166:             const cachedChannels = localStorage.getItem(cacheKey);
167:             if (cachedChannels && !forceRefresh) {
168:                 this.channels = JSON.parse(cachedChannels);
169:                 this.processChannels();
170:                 return;
171:             }
172:             
173:             const playlistUrl = this.playlists[this.selectedPlaylist] || this.playlists.global;
174:             console.log(`📡 Loading ${this.selectedPlaylist} playlist from homepage...`);
175:             const response = await fetch(playlistUrl);
176:             const playlistText = await response.text();
177:             
178:             this.parsePlaylist(playlistText);
179:             localStorage.setItem(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(this.channels));
180:             this.processChannels();
181:         } catch (error) {
182:             console.error('Error loading channels:', error);
183:             this.showErrorMessage('Failed to load channel list');
184:         }
185:     }
186: 
187:     parsePlaylist(playlistText) {
188:         const lines = playlistText.split('\n');
189:         this.channels = [];
190:         let currentChannel = null;
191: 
192:         for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
193:             const line = lines[i].trim();
194:             
195:             if (line.startsWith('#EXTINF:')) {
196:                 const metadata = this.parseChannelMetadata(line);
197:                 currentChannel = {
198:                     ...metadata,
199:                     url: '',
200:                     id: this.channels.length
201:                 };
202:             } else if (line.startsWith('http') && currentChannel) {
203:                 currentChannel.url = line;
204:                 if (this.isValidStreamUrl(line)) {
205:                     this.channels.push(currentChannel);
206:                 }
207:                 currentChannel = null;
208:             }
209:         }
210:     }
211: 
212:     parseChannelMetadata(extinfLine) {
213:         const metadata = {};
214:         
215:         // Extract channel name (everything after the last comma)
216:         const nameMatch = extinfLine.match(/,(.*)$/);
217:         metadata.name = nameMatch ? nameMatch[1].trim() : 'Unknown Channel';
218:         
219:         // Extract group/category
220:         const groupMatch = extinfLine.match(/group-title="([^"]*)"/i);
221:         metadata.group = groupMatch ? groupMatch[1] : 'General';
222:         
223:         // Extract tvg-logo
224:         const logoMatch = extinfLine.match(/tvg-logo="([^"]*)"/i);
225:         metadata.logo = logoMatch ? logoMatch[1] : '';
226:         
227:         return metadata;
228:     }
229: 
230:     isValidStreamUrl(url) {
231:         return url && (
232:             url.includes('.m3u8') || 
233:             url.includes('.mp4') || 
234:             url.includes('.ts') ||
235:             url.includes('youtube.com') ||
236:             url.includes('youtu.be')
237:         );
238:     }
239: 
240:     processChannels() {
241:         // Select featured channels (first 10)
242:         this.featuredChannels = this.channels.slice(0, 10);
243:         
244:         // Select popular channels (random selection)
245:         this.popularChannels = this.shuffleArray([...this.channels]).slice(0, 15);
246:         
247:         // Get continue watching (from localStorage or recent)
248:         this.continueWatching = this.getContinueWatching();
249:         
250:         this.renderAllSections();
251:     }

255:         if (saved) {

257:         }
258:         return this.channels.slice(0, 5); // Default to first 5 channels
259:     }

327:         this.allChannelsVisibleCount = Math.min(
328:             this.allChannelsVisibleCount + this.allChannelsPageSize,

340:     createChannelCard(channel, style = 'vertical') {
341:         const logo = channel.logo || this.getDefaultLogo(channel.group);
342:         const className = style === 'horizontal' ? 'channel-card-horizontal' : 'channel-card';
343:         const channelId = channel.id;
344:         const fallbackInitial = channel.name ? channel.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase() : 'TV';
345:         const status = this.getChannelStatus(channel.url);
346:         const statusLabel = status === 'live' ? 'LIVE' : status === 'dead' ? 'DEAD' : '...';
347:         const statusClass = status === 'live' ? 'status-live' : status === 'dead' ? 'status-dead' : 'status-checking';
348:         const channelKey = encodeURIComponent(channel.url || '');
349:         
350:         return `
351:             <div class="${className}" data-channel-id="${channelId}" data-channel-key="${channelKey}">
352:                 <div class="channel-thumbnail">
353:                     <div class="channel-placeholder" style="background: ${this.getChannelColor(channel.group)}">
354:                         <i class="fas fa-tv"></i>
355:                         <span>${fallbackInitial}</span>
356:                     </div>
357:                     ${logo
358:                         ? `<img class="channel-thumb-img" src="${logo}" alt="${channel.name} logo" onerror="this.style.display='none'">`
359:                         : ''}
360:                     <span class="channel-live-badge ${statusClass}" data-channel-key="${channelKey}">${statusLabel}</span>
361:                     <div class="channel-overlay">
362:                         <button class="play-button" data-channel-id="${channelId}">
363:                             <i class="fas fa-play"></i>
364:                         </button>
365:                     </div>
366:                 </div>
367:                 <div class="channel-info">
368:                     <h3 class="channel-name">${channel.name}</h3>
369:                     <p class="channel-group">${channel.group}</p>
370:                 </div>
371:             </div>
372:         `;
373:     }

381:             'Kids': '#2ecc71',
382:             'Movies': '#f39c12'
383:         };
384:         return colors[group] || '#6c757d';
385:     }

415:     playChannel(channelId) {
416:         const channel = this.channels.find(c => c.id == channelId);
417: 
418:         if (!channel || !channel.url) {
419:             console.error('Kanal bulunamadı veya yayın adresi boş:', channelId);
420:             alert('Kanal bulunamadı veya yayın adresi mevcut değil.');
421:             return;
422:         }
423: 
424:         this.saveContinueWatching(channel);
425: 
426:         localStorage.setItem('isa-last-channel-id', String(channel.id));
427:         localStorage.setItem('isa-last-channel-group', channel.group || 'all');
428:         localStorage.setItem('isa-last-playlist', this.selectedPlaylist || '');
429: 
430:         const queue = this.channels
431:             .filter(c => c && c.url && /^https?:\/\//i.test(c.url))
432:             .map(c => ({
433:                 name: c.name || 'Kanal',
434:                 url: c.url,
435:                 group: c.group || '',
436:                 logo: c.logo || '',
437:                 headers: c.headers || {}
438:             }));
439: 
440:         const selectedIndex = queue.findIndex(c =>
441:             c.url === channel.url && c.name === (channel.name || 'Kanal')
442:         );
443: 
444:         if (selectedIndex < 0) {
445:             alert('Kanal oynatma listesine eklenemedi.');
446:             return;
447:         }
448: 
449:         if (window.ISATV && typeof window.ISATV.play === 'function') {
450:             window.ISATV.play(
451:                 channel.name || 'Kanal',
452:                 channel.url,
453:                 JSON.stringify(channel.headers || {}),
454:                 JSON.stringify(queue),
455:                 selectedIndex
456:             );
457:         } else {
458:             console.error('Android ISATV köprüsü bulunamadı.');
459:             alert('Android oynatıcı bağlantısı bulunamadı.');
460:         }
461:     }
462: 
463: 
464:     restoreLastPlayedChannel() {
465:         const channelId = localStorage.getItem('isa-last-channel-id');
466:         const savedGroup = localStorage.getItem('isa-last-channel-group');
467: 
468:         if (channelId === null || !this.channels || !this.channels.length) return;
469: 
470:         if (savedGroup && typeof this.filterByCategory === 'function') {
471:             this.filterByCategory(savedGroup);
472:         }
473: 
474:         let attempts = 0;
475:         const reveal = () => {
476:             const card = Array.from(
477:                 document.querySelectorAll('.channel-card[data-channel-id]')
478:             ).find(el => el.dataset.channelId == channelId);
479: 
480:             if (card) {
481:                 card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
482:                 return;
483:             }
484: 
485:             if (attempts++ < 20 && typeof this.loadMoreAllChannels === 'function') {
486:                 this.loadMoreAllChannels();
487:                 setTimeout(reveal, 150);
488:             }
489:         };
490: 
491:         setTimeout(reveal, 200);
492:     }
493: 
494:     saveContinueWatching(channel) {
495:         let continueList = this.getContinueWatching();
496:         // Remove if already exists, add to front
497:         continueList = continueList.filter(c => c.id !== channel.id);
498:         continueList.unshift(channel);
499:         // Keep only last 10
500:         continueList = continueList.slice(0, 10);
501:         localStorage.setItem('continue-watching', JSON.stringify(continueList));
502:     }

516:                 stream: cleanUrl,
517:                 name: 'Custom Stream',
518:                 group: 'User Input',
519:                 playlist: this.selectedPlaylist

531: 
532:     filterChannels(searchTerm) {
533:         const filtered = this.channels.filter(channel =>
534:             channel.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
535:             channel.group.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
536:         );
537:         this.resetAllChannelsPagination(filtered);
538:     }
539: 
540:     filterByCategory(category) {
541:         const filtered = category === 'all' 
542:             ? this.channels 
543:             : this.channels.filter(channel => 
544:                 channel.group.toLowerCase().includes(category.toLowerCase())
545:             );
546:         this.resetAllChannelsPagination(filtered);
547:     }
548: 
549:     shuffleArray(array) {
550:         const shuffled = [...array];
551:         for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
552:             const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
553:             [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
554:         }
555:         return shuffled;
556:     }
557: 
558:     showErrorMessage(message) {
559:         const errorDiv = document.createElement('div');
560:         errorDiv.className = 'error-message';
561:         errorDiv.innerHTML = `
562:             <div class="error-content">
563:                 <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle"></i>
564:                 <p>${message}</p>
565:             </div>
566:         `;
567:         document.body.appendChild(errorDiv);
568:         
569:         setTimeout(() => {
570:             errorDiv.remove();
571:         }, 5000);
572:     }
573: 
574:     resolveRelayProbeBase() {
575:         const fromWindow = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? (window.STREAMFLIX_RELAY_BASE || '').trim() : '';
576:         const meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="isa-disabled-relay"]');
577:         const fromMeta = meta ? (meta.content || '').trim() : '';
578:         const raw = fromWindow || fromMeta || '';
579:         return raw.replace(/\/+$/, '');
580:     }
581: 
582:     loadChannelStatusCache() {
583:         try {
584:             const raw = localStorage.getItem(this.channelStatusCacheKey);
585:             return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
586:         } catch (error) {
587:             return {};
588:         }
589:     }
590: 
591:     saveChannelStatusCache() {
592:         try {
593:             localStorage.setItem(this.channelStatusCacheKey, JSON.stringify(this.channelStatusCache));
594:         } catch (error) {
595:             // Ignore storage errors.
596:         }
597:     }
598: 
599:     getChannelStatus(url = '') {
600:         const entry = this.channelStatusCache[url];
601:         if (!entry) return 'checking';
602:         if ((Date.now() - entry.checkedAt) > this.channelStatusTtlMs) return 'checking';
603:         return entry.state === 'live' ? 'live' : 'dead';
604:     }
605: 
606:     queueVisibleChannelStatusChecks(channels = []) {
607:         channels.forEach((channel) => {
608:             if (!channel || !channel.url) return;
609:             const cached = this.channelStatusCache[channel.url];
610:             const fresh = cached && ((Date.now() - cached.checkedAt) <= this.channelStatusTtlMs);
611:             if (fresh || this.statusProbeInFlight.has(channel.url)) return;
612:             this.statusProbeInFlight.add(channel.url);
613:             this.statusProbeQueue.push(channel.url);
614:         });
615:         this.runChannelStatusWorkers();
616:     }
617: 
618:     runChannelStatusWorkers() {
619:         while (this.statusProbeWorkers < this.maxStatusProbeWorkers && this.statusProbeQueue.length > 0) {
620:             const url = this.statusProbeQueue.shift();
621:             this.statusProbeWorkers += 1;
622:             this.probeChannelStatus(url)
623:                 .catch(() => this.setChannelStatus(url, 'dead'))
624:                 .finally(() => {
625:                     this.statusProbeWorkers -= 1;
626:                     this.statusProbeInFlight.delete(url);
627:                     this.runChannelStatusWorkers();
628:                 });
629:         }
630:     }
631: 
632:     async probeChannelStatus(url) {
633:         const target = this.buildProbeUrl(url);
634:         if (!target) {
635:             this.setChannelStatus(url, 'dead');
636:             return;
637:         }
638:         const controller = new AbortController();
639:         const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort('timeout'), 6000);
640:         try {
641:             const response = await fetch(target, { cache: 'no-store', signal: controller.signal });
642:             if (response.ok) {
643:                 this.setChannelStatus(url, 'live');
644:             } else if (this.relayEnabled && target.includes(this.relayEndpoint) && this.isHttpStreamUrl(url)) {
645:                 // If relay probe fails for HTTP stream, try direct HTTPS upgrade probe as fallback
646:                 const upgradedUrl = url.replace(/^http:\/\//i, 'https://');
647:                 const directController = new AbortController();
648:                 const directTimeout = setTimeout(() => directController.abort('timeout'), 5000);
649:                 try {
650:                     const directResponse = await fetch(upgradedUrl, { method: 'HEAD', cache: 'no-store', signal: directController.signal });
651:                     this.setChannelStatus(url, directResponse.ok ? 'live' : 'dead');
652:                 } catch (e) {
653:                     this.setChannelStatus(url, 'dead');
654:                 } finally {
655:                     clearTimeout(directTimeout);
656:                 }
657:             } else {
658:                 this.setChannelStatus(url, 'dead');
659:             }
660:         } catch (error) {
661:             this.setChannelStatus(url, 'dead');
662:         } finally {
663:             clearTimeout(timeout);
664:         }
665:     }
666: 
667:     buildProbeUrl(url) {
668:         if (!url) return null;
669:         if (this.relayProbeBase) {
670:             const separator = this.relayProbeBase.includes('?') ? '&' : '?';
671:             return `${this.relayProbeBase}${separator}url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`;
672:         }
673:         if (window.location.protocol === 'https:' && /^http:\/\//i.test(url)) {
674:             return null;
675:         }
676:         return url;
677:     }
678:
