114: window.__isaCatalogReady = function(id, totalBytes, error, notModified) {
115:     if (error) {
116:         window.__isaCatalogDone(id, null, error);
117:         return;
118:     }
119: 
120:     const callback = window.__isaCatalogCallbacks[id];
121: 
122:     if (!callback) return;
123: 
124:     if (notModified && catalogLastData &&
125:         typeof catalogLastData === 'object') {
126:         window.__isaCatalogDone(id, catalogLastData, null);
127:         return;
128:     }
129: 
130:     if (!window.ISATV ||
131:         typeof window.ISATV.readCatalogChunk !== 'function') {
132:         window.__isaCatalogDone(
133:             id, null, 'Java Catalog parça bağlantısı bulunamadı.'
134:         );
135:         return;
136:     }
137: 
138:     if (!Number.isFinite(totalBytes) || totalBytes <= 0) {
139:         window.__isaCatalogDone(
140:             id, null, 'Catalog kataloğu boş.'
141:         );
142:         return;
143:     }
144: 
145:     let offset = 0;
146:     const chunkSize = 65536;
147:     let completeText = '';
148:     const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8');
149: 
150:     function readNext() {
151:         try {
152:             const encoded = window.ISATV.readCatalogChunk(
153:                 String(offset), chunkSize
154:             );
155: 
156:             if (!encoded) {
157:                 throw new Error('Catalog veri parçası boş geldi.');
158:             }
159: 
160:             const binary = atob(encoded);
161:             const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
162: 
163:             for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
164:                 bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
165:             }
166: 
167:             offset += bytes.length;
168: 
169:             completeText += decoder.decode(
170:                 bytes,
171:                 {stream: offset < totalBytes}
172:             );
173: 
174:             const percent = Math.min(
175:                 99,
176:                 90 + Math.floor(offset * 9 / totalBytes)
177:             );
178: 
179:             window.__isaCatalogProgress(id, 'load', percent);
180: 
181:             if (offset < totalBytes) {
182:                 setTimeout(readNext, 0);
183:                 return;
184:             }
185: 
186:             completeText += decoder.decode();
187: 
188:             window.__isaCatalogDone(
189:                 id, completeText, null
190:             );
191: 
192:         } catch (e) {
193:             completeText = '';
194: 
195:             window.__isaCatalogDone(
196:                 id, null,
197:                 'Catalog aktarım hatası: ' + e.message
198:             );
199:         }
200:     }
201: 
202:     readNext();
203: };

255: function fetchCatalogCatalog() {
256:     if (catalogInFlight) return catalogInFlight;
257: 
258:     if (!window.ISATV || !window.ISATV.fetchCatalogCatalog) {
259:         return Promise.reject(
260:             new Error('Catalog native bağlantısı kullanılamıyor.')
261:         );
262:     }
263: 
264:     catalogLoading = true;
265: 
266:     catalogInFlight = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
267:         const id = ++catalogRequestId;
268: 
269:         window.__isaCatalogCallbacks[id] = {
270:             resolve: value => {
271:                 try {
272:                     const parsed = (value && typeof value === 'object')
273:                         ? value : JSON.parse(value);
274:                     if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== 'object') {
275:                         throw new Error('Boş veya geçersiz katalog.');
276:                     }
277:                     catalogLastData = parsed;
278:                     window.__isaCatalogProgress(id, "parse", 100);
279:                     setTimeout(isaCatalogHideProgress, 180);
280:                     catalogHasCache = true;
281:                     resolve(parsed);
282:                 } catch (e) {
283:                     reject(new Error('Catalog JSON doğrulaması başarısız: ' + e.message));
284:                 } finally {
285:                     catalogLoading = false;
286:                     catalogInFlight = null;
287:                 }
288:             },
289:             reject: error => {
290:                 catalogLoading = false;
291:                 catalogInFlight = null;
292:                 reject(error);
293:             }
294:         };
295: 
296:         try {
297:             window.ISATV.fetchCatalogCatalog(String(id));
298:         } catch (e) {
299:             delete window.__isaCatalogCallbacks[id];
300:             catalogLoading = false;
301:             catalogInFlight = null;
302:             reject(e);
303:         }
304:     });
305: 
306:     return catalogInFlight;
307: }

309: async function refreshCatalogCatalog(silent=false) {
310:     try {
311:         const previousData = catalogLastData;

314:         if (data === previousData) return data;
315:

422: async function getText(url,forceRefresh=false) {
423:     let cached = null;

427:     } catch(e) {}
428:

430:

432:         const fresh = await nativeFetch(url);
433:         nativeCachePut(url,fresh);

435:     } catch(e) {
436:         if (cached) return cached;
437:         throw e;
438:     }

443:         '&':'&amp;',
444:         '<':'&lt;',
445:         '>':'&gt;',
446:         '"':'&quot;',
447:         "'":'&#39;'
448:     })[c]);

473: function isDirectMedia(url) {
474:     if (!isHttp(url)) return false;
475:     try {
476:         const path = new URL(url).pathname.toLowerCase();
477:         return /\.(m3u8|mp4|mpd|mp3|m4a|aac|ts|mkv|webm|m4v|mov|ogg|flv|mpeg|mpg|3gp|avi)$/i.test(path);
478:     } catch(e) {
479:         return false;
480:     }
481: }

485: function cleanMediaTitle(value) {
486:     let name = String(value || '').trim();
487:     name = name.replace(/^(.*?)\s+-\s+\1(?=\s|$)/i, '$1').trim();
488:     // Dil/ülke etiketlerini yalnızca baştaki etiket olarak kaldır; TRT 1 gibi adlara dokunma.
489:     name = name.replace(/^(?:TR(?:\s*[:/.-]\s*|\s*🇹🇷\s*|\s+)|DE(?:\s*[:/.-]\s*|\s+|🇩🇪\s*))/iu, '');
490:     name = name.replace(/^(?:🇹🇷|🇩🇪)\s*/u, '');
491:     // Bölüm/sezon numaraları içerik başlığında tekrarlanmaz; oynatıcı alt bilgisinde gösterilir.
492:     name = name.replace(/\s*(?:[-–|·]\s*)?(?:S\s*\d{1,2}\s*E\s*\d{1,3}|Sezon\s*\d+\s*[-–:]?\s*\d+\s*\.?(?:bölüm|bolum)|\d+\s*\.?\s*(?:bölüm|bolum|episode))\s*$/i, '');
493:     return name.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/[\s|–-]+$/, '').trim();
494: }
495: function cleanGroupTitle(value) { return cleanMediaTitle(value); }
496: function cleanEpisodeTitle(value, fallbackNumber) {
497:     const name = cleanMediaTitle(value);
498:     const match = name.match(/(?:^|\s)(\d+)\s*\.?\s*(?:bölüm|bolum|episode)\s*$/i);
499:     return match ? (Number(match[1]) + '. Bölüm') : (fallbackNumber ? fallbackNumber + '. Bölüm' : name);
500: }

504: function parseM3U(text,prefix) {
505:     text = String(text || '').replace(/^\uFEFF/, '');
506: 
507:     const result = [];
508:     let current = null;
509:     let headers = {};
510:     let position = 0;
511:     let index = 0;
512: 
513:     while (position < text.length) {
514:         let end = text.indexOf('\n', position);
515:         if (end < 0) end = text.length;
516: 
517:         let line = text.slice(position, end).trim();
518:         position = end + 1;
519: 
520:         if (!line) continue;
521: 
522:         if (line.startsWith('#EXTINF:')) {
523:             const comma = line.lastIndexOf(',');
524:             const name = cleanMediaTitle(
525:                 comma >= 0 ? line.slice(comma + 1) : 'İsimsiz yayın'
526:             );
527: 
528:             const group =
529:                 (line.match(/group-title="([^"]*)"/i) || [])[1] || 'Genel';
530: 
531:             const logo =
532:                 (line.match(/tvg-logo="([^"]*)"/i) || [])[1] || '';
533: 
534:             current = {
535:                 name,
536:                 group,
537:                 logo,
538:                 url:'',
539:                 headers:{},
540:                 id:prefix + '-' + index
541:             };
542: 
543:             headers = {};
544:         }
545:         else if (line.startsWith('#EXTVLCOPT:') && current) {
546:             const option = line.substring(11);
547:             const i = option.indexOf('=');
548: 
549:             if (i > 0) {
550:                 const key = option.substring(0,i).toLowerCase();
551:                 const value = option.substring(i+1);
552: 
553:                 if (key === 'http-user-agent')
554:                     headers['User-Agent'] = value;
555: 
556:                 if (key === 'http-referrer' || key === 'http-referer')
557:                     headers['Referer'] = value;
558: 
559:                 if (key === 'http-origin')
560:                     headers['Origin'] = value;
561:             }
562:         }
563:         else if (line.startsWith('#EXTHTTP:') && current) {
564:             try {
565:                 Object.assign(headers, JSON.parse(line.substring(9)));
566:             } catch(e) {}
567:         }
568:         else if (current && !line.startsWith('#') && isHttp(line)) {
569:             current.url = line;
570:             current.headers = {...headers};
571: 
572:             result.push(current);
573:             current = null;
574:             headers = {};
575:             index++;
576:         }
577:     }
578: 
579:     return result;
580: }
581: 
582: function play(channel,queue=channels,index=-1,isLive=true) {
583:     if (!channel || !isHttp(channel.url)) return;
584: 
585:     if (window.ISATV && window.ISATV.play) {
586:         window.ISATV.play(
587:             cleanMediaTitle(channel.name || 'İSA TV HD'),
588:             channel.url,
589:             JSON.stringify(channel.headers||{}),
590:             JSON.stringify((queue||[channel]).map(item => ({...item, is_live:item.is_live !== undefined ? item.is_live : !!isLive, name:cleanMediaTitle(item.name || 'İSA TV HD')}))),
591:             index < 0 ? Math.max(0,(queue||[]).indexOf(channel)) : index
592:         );
593:         return;
594:     }
595: 
596:     const query = new URLSearchParams({
597:         stream:channel.url,
598:         name:cleanMediaTitle(channel.name||'İSA TV HD'),
599:         group:channel.group||'',
600:         logo:channel.logo||''
601:     });
602: 
603:     location.href = './player.html?'+query.toString();
604: }

607: /* ISAFLIX_QUEUE_V2 */
608: function playIsaFlix(name, url, headers={}, queue=[], index=0) {
609:     if (!isHttp(url)) {
610:         alert('Geçerli video bağlantısı bulunamadı.');
611:         return;
612:     }
613: 
614:     const list = Array.isArray(queue) && queue.length
615:         ? queue
616:         : [{
617:             name:name || 'İSAFLIX',
618:             url:url,
619:             headers:headers || {},
620:             season:0,
621:             episode:0,
622:             is_live:false
623:         }];
624: 
625:     let selected = Number(index);
626:     if (!Number.isInteger(selected) || selected < 0 || selected >= list.length) {
627:         selected = list.findIndex(x => x.url === url);
628:     }
629:     if (selected < 0) selected = 0;
630: 
631:     if (window.ISATV &&
632:         typeof window.ISATV.playIsaflix === 'function') {
633:         window.ISATV.playIsaflix(
634:             cleanMediaTitle(name || 'İSAFLIX'),
635:             url,
636:             JSON.stringify(headers || {}),
637:             JSON.stringify(list),
638:             selected
639:         );
640:         return;
641:     }
642: 
643:     play({
644:         name:name || 'İSAFLIX',
645:         url:url,
646:         logo:'',
647:         group:'İSAFLIX',
648:         headers:headers || {}
649:     }, list, selected, false);
650: }
651: 
652: function openPage(url) {
653:     if (!isHttp(url)) return;
654: 
655:     if (window.ISATV && window.ISATV.openPage) {
656:         window.ISATV.openPage(url);
657:         return;
658:     }
659: 
660:     window.open(url,'_blank','noopener');
661: }

663: function renderChannels() {
664:     const grid = document.getElementById('all-channels');
665:     if (!grid) return;
666: 
667:     grid.innerHTML = '';
668: 
669:     if (!channels.length) {
670:         grid.innerHTML = '<p class="isa-loading">Henüz kanal bulunamadı.</p>';
671:         renderFavorites();
672:         return;
673:     }
674: 
675:     const PAGE_SIZE = 500;
676:     const groupCounts = new Map();
677: 
678:     for (let i = 0; i < channels.length; i++) {
679:         const group = cleanGroupTitle(channels[i].group || 'Genel');
680:         groupCounts.set(group, (groupCounts.get(group) || 0) + 1);
681:     }
682: 
683:     const groupNodes = new Map();
684: 
685:     function getGroupGrid(group) {
686:         if (groupNodes.has(group)) return groupNodes.get(group);
687: 
688:         const details = document.createElement('details');
689:         details.className = 'isa-channel-category';
690: 
691:         const summary = document.createElement('summary');
692:         summary.textContent = group + ' (' + groupCounts.get(group) + ')';
693: 
694:         const list = document.createElement('div');
695:         list.className = 'channels-grid';
696: 
697:         details.appendChild(summary);
698:         details.appendChild(list);
699:         grid.appendChild(details);
700: 
701:         groupNodes.set(group, list);
702:         return list;
703:     }
704: 
705:     let shown = 0;
706:     let moreButton = null;
707: 
708:     function appendNextPage() {
709:         if (moreButton) {
710:             moreButton.remove();
711:             moreButton = null;
712:         }
713: 
714:         const end = Math.min(shown + PAGE_SIZE, channels.length);
715: 
716:         for (let index = shown; index < end; index++) {
717:             const channel = channels[index];
718:             const group = cleanGroupTitle(channel.group || 'Genel');
719:             const list = getGroupGrid(group);
720: 
721:             const card = document.createElement('div');
722:             card.className = 'channel-card';
723:             card.dataset.isaIndex = String(index);
724: 
725:             const thumbnail = document.createElement('div');
726:             thumbnail.className = 'channel-thumbnail';
727: 
728:             if (channel.logo) {
729:                 const img = document.createElement('img');
730:                 img.src = channel.logo;
731:                 img.alt = '';
732:                 img.onerror = function () {
733:                     this.style.display = 'none';
734:                 };
735:                 thumbnail.appendChild(img);
736:             } else {
737:                 const placeholder = document.createElement('div');
738:                 placeholder.className = 'channel-placeholder';
739:                 placeholder.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-tv"></i>';
740:                 thumbnail.appendChild(placeholder);
741:             }
742: 
743:             const name = document.createElement('div');
744:             name.className = 'channel-name';
745:             name.textContent = cleanMediaTitle(channel.name || 'İsimsiz kanal');
746: 
747:             card.appendChild(thumbnail);
748:             card.appendChild(name);
749: 
750:             card.addEventListener('click', function () {
751:                 play(channels[index], channels, index);
752:             });
753: 
754:             list.appendChild(card);
755:         }
756: 
757:         shown = end;
758: 
759:         if (shown < channels.length) {
760:             moreButton = document.createElement('button');
761:             moreButton.type = 'button';
762:             moreButton.className = 'isa-load-more';
763:             moreButton.textContent =
764:                 'Daha Fazla Kanal Yükle (' + shown + ' / ' +
765:                 channels.length + ')';
766: 
767:             moreButton.style.display = 'block';
768:             moreButton.style.width = '100%';
769:             moreButton.style.padding = '14px';
770:             moreButton.style.margin = '16px 0';
771:             moreButton.style.cursor = 'pointer';
772: 
773:             moreButton.addEventListener('click', appendNextPage);
774:             grid.appendChild(moreButton);
775:         }
776:     }
777: 
778:     appendNextPage();
779:     renderFavorites();
780: }
781: function renderFavorites() {
782:     const section = document.getElementById('isa-favorites-section');
783:     const grid = document.getElementById('isa-favorites-grid');
784:     const toggle = document.getElementById('isa-favorites-toggle');
785:     if (!section || !grid || !toggle) return;
786:     let favorites = [];
787:     try {
788:         if (window.ISATV && window.ISATV.getFavorites) favorites = JSON.parse(window.ISATV.getFavorites() || '[]');
789:     } catch(e) { console.warn('Favoriler okunamadı:', e); }
790:     if (!Array.isArray(favorites)) favorites = [];
791:     if (!favorites.length) {
792:         isaFavoritesOpen = false;
793:         section.hidden = true;
794:         toggle.hidden = true;
795:         toggle.style.display = 'none';
796:         toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
797:         grid.innerHTML = '';
798:         return;
799:     }
800:     toggle.hidden = false;
801:     toggle.style.display = 'block';
802:     if (!isaFavoritesToggleBound) {
803:         toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
804:             isaFavoritesOpen = !isaFavoritesOpen;
805:             section.hidden = !isaFavoritesOpen;
806:             toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isaFavoritesOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
807:             toggle.innerHTML = isaFavoritesOpen ? '♥ Favoriler ▴' : '♥ Favoriler ▾';
808:         });
809:         isaFavoritesToggleBound = true;
810:     }
811:     section.hidden = !isaFavoritesOpen;
812:     toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isaFavoritesOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
813:     toggle.innerHTML = isaFavoritesOpen ? '♥ Favoriler ▴' : '♥ Favoriler ▾';
814:     grid.innerHTML = favorites.map((item,index) => `
815:         <div class="channel-card isa-favorite-card" data-favorite-index="${index}">
816:             <div class="channel-thumbnail">${item.logo ? `<img src="${esc(item.logo)}" alt="" loading="lazy" onerror="this.style.display='none'">` : '<div class="channel-placeholder"><i class="fas fa-tv"></i></div>'}</div>
817:             <div class="channel-name">${esc(cleanMediaTitle(item.name || 'İsimsiz içerik'))}</div>
818:         </div>`).join('');
819:     grid.querySelectorAll('[data-favorite-index]').forEach(card => card.addEventListener('click', () => {
820:         const item = favorites[Number(card.dataset.favoriteIndex)];
821:         if (!item || !item.url) return;
822:         const index = channels.findIndex(c => c.url === item.url);
823:         if (index >= 0) play(channels[index],channels,index,true);
824:         else play(item,favorites,Number(card.dataset.favoriteIndex),item.is_live !== false);
825:     }));
826: }
827: 
828: function renderSavedLists() {
829:     const compact = document.getElementById('isa-saved-lists');
830:     const full = document.getElementById('isa-all-saved-lists');
831:     const makeRows = () => playlists.map((item,index) => {
832:         const type = item.type === 'xtream' ? 'Xtream Codes' : item.type === 'mag' ? 'MAG' : 'M3U';
833:         const detail = item.type === 'xtream' ? item.host + ' • ' + item.username : item.type === 'mag' ? item.host + ' • ' + item.mac : (item.url || 'Cihazdan yüklenen dosya');
834:         return `<div class="isa-list-row"><span class="isa-list-info"><strong>${esc(item.name || ('Liste '+(index+1)))}</strong><small>${esc(type)} · ${Number(item.lastCount || 0)} içerik</small><small>${esc(detail)}</small></span><span class="isa-list-actions"><button data-refresh-list="${index}" title="Yenile"><i class="fas fa-rotate"></i></button><button class="delete" data-delete-list="${index}" title="Sil"><i class="fas fa-trash"></i></button></span></div>`;
835:     }).join('');
836:     if (compact) {
837:         if (!playlists.length) compact.innerHTML = '<p class="isa-vod-note">Henüz kaynak eklenmedi.</p>';
838:         else {
839:             const active = playlists[playlists.length-1];
840:             const n = playlists.length;
841:             compact.innerHTML = `<details class="isa-active-list"><summary><span><b>AKTİF LİSTE</b><strong>${esc(active.name || ('Liste '+n))}</strong><small>${Number(active.lastCount || 0)} içerik · ${active.type==='xtream'?'Xtream':active.type==='mag'?'MAG':'M3U'}</small></span><i class="fas fa-chevron-down"></i></summary><div class="isa-account-menu">${makeRows()}</div></details>`;
842:         }
843:     }
844:     if (full) full.innerHTML = makeRows() || '<p class="isa-vod-note">Henüz kaynak eklenmedi.</p>';
845:     [compact,full].filter(Boolean).forEach(box => {
846:         box.querySelectorAll('[data-refresh-list]').forEach(button => button.onclick = async e => {
847:             e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); sourceLoading(true,'Kaynak yenileniyor...');
848:             try { await refreshAll(true); } finally { sourceLoading(false); }
849:         });
850:         box.querySelectorAll('[data-delete-list]').forEach(button => button.onclick = async e => {
851:             e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation(); const index=Number(button.dataset.deleteList); if(!playlists[index]) return;
852:             if(!confirm((playlists[index].name||'Liste')+' silinsin mi?')) return;
853:             playlists.splice(index,1); saveLists();
854:             sourceLoading(true,'Kaynaklar güncelleniyor...'); try { await refreshAll(false); } finally { sourceLoading(false); }
855:         });
856:     });
857: }

865: async function loadUserLists(forceRefresh=false) {
866:     const result = [];
867: 
868:     for (let index=0; index<playlists.length; index++) {
869:         const item = playlists[index];
870: 
871:         try {
872:             if (item.type === 'xtream') {
873:                 const live = await xtreamAPI(item, 'get_live_streams');
874: 
875:                 const categories = await xtreamAPI(
876:                     item, 'get_live_categories'
877:                 ).catch(() => []);
878: 
879:                 const categoryMap = new Map(
880:                     (Array.isArray(categories) ? categories : []).map(c =>
881:                         [String(c.category_id), c.category_name || 'Genel']
882:                     )
883:                 );
884: 
885:                 for (const stream of (Array.isArray(live) ? live : [])) {
886:                     const ext = stream.container_extension || 'ts';
887:                     const url = item.host.replace(/\/+$/, '') +
888:                         '/live/' +
889:                         encodeURIComponent(item.username) + '/' +
890:                         encodeURIComponent(item.password) + '/' +
891:                         stream.stream_id + '.' + ext;
892: 
893:                     result.push({
894:                         name:stream.name || 'İsimsiz kanal',
895:                         url,
896:                         logo:stream.stream_icon || '',
897:                         group:categoryMap.get(
898:                             String(stream.category_id)
899:                         ) || 'Genel',
900:                         headers:{},
901:                         id:'xtream-live-' + index + '-' + stream.stream_id
902:                     });
903:                 }
904: 
905:                 item.lastCount = live.length;
906:                 item.error = '';
907:                 continue;
908:             }
909: 
910:             if (item.type === 'mag') {
911:                 const magChannels = await loadMagChannels(item);
912: 
913:                 for (const channel of magChannels) {
914:                     result.push(channel);
915:                 }
916: 
917:                 item.lastCount = magChannels.length;
918:                 item.error = '';
919:                 continue;
920:             }
921: 
922:             if (item.type === 'url' || item.type === 'm3u-url') {
923:                 item.text = await getText(item.url, forceRefresh);
924:             }
925: 
926:             if (!item.text ||
927:                 (!item.text.includes('#EXTM3U') &&
928:                  !item.text.includes('#EXTINF:'))) {
929:                 throw new Error('Geçerli M3U içeriği bulunamadı.');
930:             }
931: 
932:             const parsed = parseM3U(
933:                 item.text,
934:                 'user-' + index
935:             );
936: 
937:             item.lastCount = parsed.length;
938:             item.error = '';
939: 
940:             // Dosya adı artık grup adına eklenmez.
941:             for (const channel of parsed) {
942:                 channel.group = channel.group || 'Genel';
943:                 result.push(channel);
944:             }
945: 
946:         } catch(error) {
947:             item.error = error.message || 'Kaynak yüklenemedi.';
948:             console.warn(item.name, error);
949:         }
950:     }
951: 
952:     saveLists();
953:     return result;
954: }
955: 
956: 
957: 
958: function xtreamAPI(source, action, extra={}) {
959:     const host = String(source.host || '').replace(/\/+$/, '');
960: 
961:     const params = new URLSearchParams({
962:         username:source.username,
963:         password:source.password,
964:         action,
965:         ...extra
966:     });
967: 
968:     return getText(
969:         host + '/player_api.php?' + params.toString(),
970:         true
971:     ).then(text => {
972:         const data = JSON.parse(text);
973: 
974:         if (data && data.user_info &&
975:             data.user_info.auth === 0) {
976:             throw new Error('Xtream kullanıcı bilgileri geçersiz.');
977:         }
978: 
979:         if (data && data.user_info &&
980:             data.user_info.status === 'Disabled') {
981:             throw new Error('Xtream hesabı devre dışı.');
982:         }
983: 
984:         return data;
985:     });
986: }
987: 
988: function sourceHost(value) {
989:     let host = String(value || '').trim();
990: 
991:     if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(host)) {
992:         host = 'http://' + host;
993:     }
994: 
995:     const parsed = new URL(host);
996: 
997:     if (!['http:', 'https:'].includes(parsed.protocol)) {
998:         throw new Error('Geçerli HTTP/HTTPS host girin.');
999:     }
1000: 
1001:     return {
1002:         host:parsed.origin + parsed.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, ''),
1003:         hasPort:!!parsed.port
1004:     };
1005: }
1006: 
1007: function sourceLoading(show, message) {
1008:     const box = document.getElementById('isa-source-loading');
1009:     const text = document.getElementById('isa-loading-text');
1010:     if (box) box.classList.toggle('active', !!show);
1011:     if (text && message) text.textContent = message;
1012: }
1013: 
1014: function nextSourceName(type) {
1015:     const prefix = type === 'xtream' ? 'Xtream' :
1016:                    type === 'mag' ? 'MAG' : 'M3U';
1017: 
1018:     let n = 1;
1019:     while (playlists.some(p => p.name === prefix + ' ' + n)) n++;
1020:     return prefix + ' ' + n;
1021: }
1022: 
1023: async function loadMagChannels(source) {
1024:     const host = String(source.host || '').replace(/\/+$/, '');
1025:     const mac = String(source.mac || '').trim().toUpperCase();
1026:     if (!host || !/^([0-9A-F]{2}:){5}[0-9A-F]{2}$/.test(mac)) {
1027:         throw new Error('MAG host veya MAC adresi geçersiz.');
1028:     }
1029: 
1030:     const portal = host + '/portal.php';
1031:     const baseHeaders = {
1032:         'User-Agent':'Mozilla/5.0 (QtEmbedded; U; Linux; C) AppleWebKit/533.3',
1033:         'X-User-Agent':'Model: MAG250; Link: WiFi',
1034:         'Referer':host + '/c/',
1035:         'Origin':new URL(host).origin,
1036:         'Cookie':'mac=' + mac + '; stb_lang=en; timezone=Europe/Istanbul',
1037:         'Accept':'*/*'
1038:     };
1039: 
1040:     async function portalRequest(params, token='') {
1041:         const query = new URLSearchParams({
1042:             ...params,
1043:             JsHttpRequest:'1-xml',
1044:             mac:mac
1045:         });
1046:         const headers = {...baseHeaders};
1047:         if (token) headers.Authorization = 'Bearer ' + token;
1048:         const raw = await getTextWithHeaders(portal + '?' + query.toString(),headers);
1049:         let response;
1050:         try { response = JSON.parse(raw); }
1051:         catch (e) { throw new Error('MAG portalı geçerli JSON döndürmedi.'); }
1052:         if (response && response.js && response.js.error) {
1053:             throw new Error(String(response.js.error));
1054:         }
1055:         return response;
1056:     }
1057: 
1058:     const handshake = await portalRequest({type:'stb',action:'handshake',token:''});
1059:     const token = handshake && handshake.js && handshake.js.token
1060:         ? String(handshake.js.token) : '';
1061:     if (!token) {
1062:         throw new Error('MAG handshake token alınamadı. MAC ve portal erişimini kontrol edin.');
1063:     }
1064: 
1065:     try {
1066:         await portalRequest({type:'stb',action:'get_profile'},token);
1067:     } catch (e) {}
1068: 
1069:     let genres = [];
1070:     try {
1071:         const genreResponse = await portalRequest({type:'itv',action:'get_genres'},token);
1072:         const genreData = genreResponse && genreResponse.js
1073:             ? (genreResponse.js.data || genreResponse.js) : [];
1074:         genres = Array.isArray(genreData) ? genreData : [];
1075:     } catch (e) {}
1076: 
1077:     const genreMap = new Map();
1078:     for (const genre of genres) {
1079:         const id = genre.id ?? genre.genre_id ?? genre.tv_genre_id;
1080:         const name = genre.title || genre.name || genre.genre_name;
1081:         if (id != null && name) genreMap.set(String(id),String(name));
1082:     }
1083: 
1084:     const channelResponse = await portalRequest({
1085:         type:'itv',action:'get_all_channels',force_ch_link_check:''
1086:     },token);
1087: 
1088:     let rows = channelResponse && channelResponse.js
1089:         ? channelResponse.js.data : [];
1090:     if (rows && !Array.isArray(rows) && Array.isArray(rows.data)) rows = rows.data;
1091:     if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) {
1092:         throw new Error('MAG portalı kanal listesi döndürmedi.');
1093:     }
1094: 
1095:     function cleanCommand(value) {
1096:         return String(value || '').trim().replace(/^(ffmpeg|auto)\s+/i,'').trim();
1097:     }
1098: 
1099:     async function resolveMagLink(row) {
1100:         const cmd = String(row.cmd || row.url || '').trim();
1101:         const direct = cleanCommand(cmd);
1102:         if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(direct)) return direct;
1103:         if (!cmd) return '';
1104: 
1105:         const linkResponse = await portalRequest({
1106:             type:'itv',
1107:             action:'create_link',
1108:             cmd:cmd,
1109:             series:'0',
1110:             forced_storage:'',
1111:             disable_ad:'0'
1112:         },token);
1113: 
1114:         const data = linkResponse && linkResponse.js ? linkResponse.js : {};
1115:         const value = cleanCommand(
1116:             data.cmd || data.url ||
1117:             (data.data && (data.data.cmd || data.data.url)) || ''
1118:         );
1119:         return /^https?:\/\//i.test(value) ? value : '';
1120:     }
1121: 
1122:     const result = [];
1123:     // Portalı aşırı yüklememek için dörder kanal halinde çözümlenir.
1124:     for (let offset = 0; offset < rows.length; offset += 4) {
1125:         const batch = rows.slice(offset,offset + 4);
1126:         const resolved = await Promise.all(batch.map(async (row,localIndex) => {
1127:             try {
1128:                 const url = await resolveMagLink(row);
1129:                 if (!url) return null;
1130:                 const genreId = row.tv_genre_id ?? row.genre_id ?? row.category_id ?? '';
1131:                 return {
1132:                     name:row.name || row.title || 'MAG Kanal ' + (offset + localIndex + 1),
1133:                     url:url,
1134:                     logo:row.logo || row.icon || '',
1135:                     group:row.tv_genre_name || genreMap.get(String(genreId)) || 'MAG',
1136:                     headers:{...baseHeaders,Authorization:'Bearer ' + token},
1137:                     id:'mag-' + mac.replace(/:/g,'') + '-' + (offset + localIndex)
1138:                 };
1139:             } catch (error) {
1140:                 return null;
1141:             }
1142:         }));
1143:         for (const item of resolved) {
1144:             if (item) result.push(item);
1145:         }
1146:     }
1147: 
1148:     if (!result.length) {
1149:         throw new Error('MAG portalından oynatılabilir yayın alınamadı. Portal create_link desteği vermiyor olabilir.');
1150:     }
1151:     return result;
1152: }
1153: 
1154: async function detectXtreamM3U(url) {
1155:     try {
1156:         const parsed = new URL(url);
1157:         const username = parsed.searchParams.get('username');
1158:         const password = parsed.searchParams.get('password');
1159: 
1160:         if (!username || !password) return null;
1161: 
1162:         const source = {
1163:             host:parsed.origin,
1164:             username,
1165:             password
1166:         };
1167: 
1168:         const data = await xtreamAPI(
1169:             source,
1170:             'get_live_categories'
1171:         );
1172: 
1173:         if (!Array.isArray(data)) return null;
1174: 
1175:         return source;
1176:     } catch(error) {
1177:         return null;
1178:     }
1179: }
1180: 
1181: async function addM3UUrl(url) {
1182:     const xtream = await detectXtreamM3U(url);
1183: 
1184:     if (xtream) {
1185:         const choice = confirm(
1186:             'Bu adres Xtream Codes desteği sunuyor.\\n\\n' +
1187:             'Tam Xtream sistemiyle film ve dizileri de yüklemek için ' +
1188:             'Tamam seçin.\\n\\n' +
1189:             'Yalnızca düz M3U kanalları için İptal seçin.'
1190:         );
1191: 
1192:         if (choice) {
1193:             playlists.push({
1194:                 ...xtream,
1195:                 type:'xtream',
1196:                 name:nextSourceName('xtream')
1197:             });
1198:         } else {
1199:             playlists.push({
1200:                 type:'url',
1201:                 name:nextSourceName('m3u'),
1202:                 url,
1203:                 text:''
1204:             });
1205:         }
1206:     } else {
1207:         playlists.push({
1208:             type:'url',
1209:             name:nextSourceName('m3u'),
1210:             url,
1211:             text:''
1212:         });
1213:     }
1214: 
1215:     saveLists();
1216:     await refreshAll(false);
1217: }
1218: 
1219: function providerName(provider) {
1220:     return provider.name || provider.title || provider.provider || 'VOD Kaynağı';
1221: }
1222: 
1223: 
1224: async function loadVOD(forceRefresh=false) {
1225:     vodProviders = [];
1226: 
1227:     for (const source of playlists) {
1228:         if (source.type === 'xtream') {
1229:             vodProviders.push({
1230:                 ...source,
1231:                 type:'xtream',
1232:                 title:source.name
1233:             });
1234:         }
1235:     }
1236: 
1237:     const grid = document.getElementById('isa-vod-grid');
1238:     const status = document.getElementById('isa-vod-status');
1239: 
1240:     if (grid) {
1241:         grid.innerHTML = vodProviders.map((provider,index) => `
1242:             <button class="isa-provider-card"
1243:                     data-xtream-provider="${index}">
1244:                 <i class="fas fa-server" style="font-size:32px;padding:20px"></i>
1245:                 <strong>${esc(provider.name)}</strong>
1246:                 <small>Xtream Codes</small>
1247:             </button>
1248:         `).join('');
1249: 
1250:         grid.querySelectorAll('[data-xtream-provider]').forEach(button => {
1251:             button.onclick = () => openVODProvider(
1252:                 vodProviders[Number(button.dataset.xtreamProvider)]
1253:             );
1254:         });
1255:     }
1256: 
1257:     if (status) {
1258:         status.textContent = vodProviders.length +
1259:             ' Xtream VOD kaynağı hazır.';
1260:     }
1261: }
1262: 
1263: 
1264: /*
1265:  * JSON nesnelerini düzleştirmeden korur.
1266:  * Dizi nesnesinin episodes/seasons yapısı kaybolmaz.
1267:  */
1268: 
1269: function normalizeVOD(data,result=[]) {
1270:     if (Array.isArray(data)) {
1271:         data.forEach(item => normalizeVOD(item,result));
1272:         return result;
1273:     }
1274: 
1275:     if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') return result;
1276: 
1277:     const title = data.title || data.name || data.baslik ||
1278:                   data.titleName || data.filename || data.original_title;
1279: 
1280:     const url = data.url || data.link || data.file ||
1281:                 data.stream || data.playUrl || data.video ||
1282:                 data.videoUrl || data.video_url || data.embedUrl || '';
1283: 
1284:     const episodes = Array.isArray(data.episodes) ? data.episodes : [];
1285:     const seasons = Array.isArray(data.seasons) ? data.seasons : [];
1286:     const items = Array.isArray(data.items) ? data.items : [];
1287: 
1288:     if (title && (url || episodes.length || seasons.length || items.length ||
1289:                   String(data.type || '').toLowerCase().includes('dizi') ||
1290:                   String(data.type || '').toLowerCase().includes('series'))) {
1291:         result.push({
1292:             type:String(data.type || '').toLowerCase(),
1293:             name:String(title),
1294:             title:String(title),
1295:             url:url,
1296:             videoUrl:data.videoUrl || data.video_url || '',
1297:             logo:data.poster || data.image || data.cover ||
1298:                  data.thumbnail || data.thumb || '',
1299:             poster:data.poster || data.image || data.cover ||
1300:                    data.thumbnail || '',
1301:             description:data.description || '',
1302:             category:data.category || '',
1303:             genres:Array.isArray(data.genres) ? data.genres : [],
1304:             year:data.year || '',
1305:             platform:data.platform || '',
1306:             episodes:episodes,
1307:             seasons:seasons,
1308:             items:items,
1309:             raw:data
1310:         });
1311:         return result;
1312:     }
1313: 
1314:     Object.values(data).forEach(value => {
1315:         if (value && typeof value === 'object') normalizeVOD(value,result);
1316:     });
1317: 
1318:     return result;
1319: }
1320:

1324: function itemIsSeries(item) {
1325:     return (Array.isArray(item.episodes) && item.episodes.length > 0) ||
1326:            (Array.isArray(item.seasons) && item.seasons.length > 0) ||
1327:            String(item.type || '').includes('dizi') ||
1328:            String(item.type || '').includes('series');
1329: }

1333: function showVODProviders() {
1334:     const grid = document.getElementById('isa-vod-grid');
1335:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1336:     const heading = document.querySelector('#isa-vod-panel .isa-section-heading');
1337: 
1338:     grid.style.display = '';
1339:     area.innerHTML = '';
1340: 
1341:     if (heading) heading.style.display = '';
1342: 
1343:     document.getElementById('isa-vod-status').textContent =
1344:         vodProviders.length + ' VOD kaynağı hazır.';
1345: }

1347: 
1348: function renderVODItems(items,provider) {
1349:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1350: 
1351:     const films = [];
1352:     const series = [];
1353: 
1354:     for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
1355:         const entry = {item:items[i], index:i};
1356: 
1357:         if (itemIsSeries(items[i])) series.push(entry);
1358:         else films.push(entry);
1359:     }
1360: 
1361:     area.innerHTML = `
1362:         <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1363:             <button class="isa-back" id="isa-vod-back">
1364:                 <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Kaynaklar
1365:             </button>
1366:             <h2>${esc(providerName(provider))}</h2>
1367:         </div>
1368:         ${series.length ? '<h3>Diziler</h3><div id="isa-series-grid" class="isa-provider-grid"></div>' : ''}
1369:         ${films.length ? '<h3 style="margin-top:25px">Filmler ve diğer içerikler</h3><div id="isa-films-grid" class="isa-provider-grid"></div>' : ''}
1370:         ${!items.length ? '<p class="isa-vod-note">Bu kaynakta gösterilebilir içerik bulunamadı.</p>' : ''}
1371:     `;
1372: 
1373:     document.getElementById('isa-vod-back').onclick = showVODProviders;
1374: 
1375:     const renderBatch = (entries, gridId) => {
1376:         const grid = document.getElementById(gridId);
1377:         if (!grid) return;
1378: 
1379:         let offset = 0;
1380:         const batchSize = 100;
1381: 
1382:         const next = () => {
1383:             if (!grid.isConnected) return;
1384: 
1385:             const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
1386:             const limit = Math.min(offset + batchSize, entries.length);
1387: 
1388:             for (; offset < limit; offset++) {
1389:                 const entry = entries[offset];
1390:                 const item = entry.item;
1391:                 const kind = itemIsSeries(item) ? 'series' : 'film';
1392: 
1393:                 const holder = document.createElement('div');
1394:                 holder.innerHTML = vodCard(item,kind,entry.index);
1395: 
1396:                 const button = holder.firstElementChild;
1397:                 button.addEventListener('click', () => {
1398:                     if (kind === 'series') {
1399:                         if (provider.type === 'xtream' && item.xtream)
1400:                             openXtreamSeries(item,provider);
1401:                         else
1402:                             showSeries(item,provider);
1403:                     } else {
1404:                         openVODItem(item);
1405:                     }
1406:                 });
1407: 
1408:                 fragment.appendChild(button);
1409:             }
1410: 
1411:             grid.appendChild(fragment);
1412: 
1413:             if (offset < entries.length) {
1414:                 requestAnimationFrame(next);
1415:             }
1416:         };
1417: 
1418:         requestAnimationFrame(next);
1419:     };
1420: 
1421:     renderBatch(series,'isa-series-grid');
1422:     renderBatch(films,'isa-films-grid');
1423: }
1424: 
1425: function vodCard(item,kind,index) {
1426:     return `
1427:         <button class="isa-provider-card" data-vod-kind="${kind}" data-vod-index="${index}">
1428:             ${item.logo ? `<img src="${esc(item.logo)}" alt="" onerror="this.style.display='none'">` : '<i class="fas fa-film" style="font-size:35px;padding:25px"></i>'}
1429:             <strong>${esc(item.name)}</strong>
1430:             <small>${esc(item.year)} ${esc(item.category)}</small>
1431:         </button>
1432:     `;
1433: }

1435: 
1436: async function openVODProvider(provider) {
1437:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1438:     const grid = document.getElementById('isa-vod-grid');
1439:     const heading = document.querySelector('#isa-vod-panel .isa-section-heading');
1440: 
1441:     currentProvider = provider;
1442:     if (grid) grid.style.display = 'none';
1443:     if (heading) heading.style.display = 'none';
1444:     area.innerHTML = '<p class="isa-loading">İçerikler yükleniyor...</p>';
1445: 
1446:     try {
1447:         let items = [];
1448:         if (provider.type === 'xtream') {
1449:             const [films,series,filmCategories,seriesCategories] = await Promise.all([
1450:                 xtreamAPI(provider,'get_vod_streams'),
1451:                 xtreamAPI(provider,'get_series'),
1452:                 xtreamAPI(provider,'get_vod_categories').catch(() => []),
1453:                 xtreamAPI(provider,'get_series_categories').catch(() => [])
1454:             ]);
1455: 
1456:             const filmMap = new Map();
1457:             for (const category of (Array.isArray(filmCategories) ? filmCategories : [])) {
1458:                 filmMap.set(String(category.category_id),category.category_name || 'Filmler');
1459:             }
1460:             const seriesMap = new Map();
1461:             for (const category of (Array.isArray(seriesCategories) ? seriesCategories : [])) {
1462:                 seriesMap.set(String(category.category_id),category.category_name || 'Diziler');
1463:             }
1464: 
1465:             for (const item of (Array.isArray(films) ? films : [])) {
1466:                 items.push({
1467:                     ...item,
1468:                     type:'film',
1469:                     name:item.name || 'Film',
1470:                     title:item.name || 'Film',
1471:                     category:filmMap.get(String(item.category_id)) || 'Filmler',
1472:                     url:provider.host.replace(/\/+$/,'') +
1473:                         '/movie/' + encodeURIComponent(provider.username) + '/' +
1474:                         encodeURIComponent(provider.password) + '/' +
1475:                         item.stream_id + '.' + (item.container_extension || 'mp4'),
1476:                     logo:item.stream_icon || item.cover || '',
1477:                     raw:item
1478:                 });
1479:             }
1480: 
1481:             for (const item of (Array.isArray(series) ? series : [])) {
1482:                 items.push({
1483:                     ...item,
1484:                     type:'series',
1485:                     name:item.name || 'Dizi',
1486:                     title:item.name || 'Dizi',
1487:                     category:seriesMap.get(String(item.category_id)) || 'Diziler',
1488:                     url:'',
1489:                     logo:item.cover || item.stream_icon || '',
1490:                     raw:item,
1491:                     xtream:true
1492:                 });
1493:             }
1494:         } else {
1495:             const text = await getText(provider.url);
1496:             if (text.trim().startsWith('#EXTM3U') || text.includes('#EXTINF:')) {
1497:                 items = parseM3U(text,'vod').map(item => ({
1498:                     ...item,title:item.name,logo:item.logo,episodes:[],seasons:[],type:'film'
1499:                 }));
1500:             } else {
1501:                 items = normalizeVOD(JSON.parse(text));
1502:             }
1503:         }
1504:         renderVODItems(items,provider);
1505:     } catch(error) {
1506:         area.innerHTML = `
1507:             <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1508:                 <button class="isa-back" id="isa-vod-error-back">Geri</button>
1509:                 <h2>${esc(providerName(provider))}</h2>
1510:             </div>
1511:             <p class="isa-vod-note">İçerik alınamadı: ${esc(error.message)}</p>
1512:         `;
1513:         const back = document.getElementById('isa-vod-error-back');
1514:         if (back) back.onclick = showVODProviders;
1515:     }
1516: }
1517: 
1518: async function openXtreamSeries(series,provider) {
1519:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1520:     area.innerHTML = '<p class="isa-loading">Dizi bölümleri alınıyor...</p>';
1521: 
1522:     try {
1523:         const info = await xtreamAPI(provider, 'get_series_info', {
1524:             series_id:series.series_id
1525:         });
1526: 
1527:         const rawEpisodes = info.episodes || {};
1528:         const seasons = info.seasons || [];
1529:         const episodes = [];
1530: 
1531:         if (Array.isArray(rawEpisodes)) {
1532:             for (const episode of rawEpisodes) episodes.push(episode);
1533:         } else {
1534:             Object.values(rawEpisodes).forEach(list => {
1535:                 if (Array.isArray(list)) { for (const episode of list) episodes.push(episode); }
1536:             });
1537:         }
1538: 
1539:         const grouped = new Map();
1540: 
1541:         for (const episode of episodes) {
1542:             const season = Number(
1543:                 episode.season || episode.season_number || 1
1544:             );
1545: 
1546:             if (!grouped.has(season)) grouped.set(season, []);
1547: 
1548:             const ext = episode.container_extension || 'mp4';
1549: 
1550:             grouped.get(season).push({
1551:                 ...episode,
1552:                 name:episode.title || episode.name || 'Bölüm',
1553:                 title:episode.title || episode.name || 'Bölüm',
1554:                 url:provider.host.replace(/\/+$/, '') +
1555:                     '/series/' +
1556:                     encodeURIComponent(provider.username) + '/' +
1557:                     encodeURIComponent(provider.password) + '/' +
1558:                     episode.id + '.' + ext
1559:             });
1560:         }
1561: 
1562:         const seasonNumbers = Array.from(grouped.keys()).sort((a,b) => a-b);
1563: 
1564:         area.innerHTML = `
1565:             <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1566:                 <button class="isa-back" id="isa-xtream-series-back">
1567:                     <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Geri
1568:                 </button>
1569:                 <h2>${esc(series.name)}</h2>
1570:             </div>
1571:             <div class="isa-provider-grid">
1572:                 ${seasonNumbers.map((number,index) => `
1573:                     <button class="isa-provider-card"
1574:                             data-xseason="${index}">
1575:                         <i class="fas fa-layer-group"
1576:                            style="font-size:32px;padding:20px"></i>
1577:                         <strong>Sezon ${number}</strong>
1578:                         <small>${grouped.get(number).length} bölüm</small>
1579:                     </button>
1580:                 `).join('')}
1581:             </div>
1582:         `;
1583: 
1584:         document.getElementById('isa-xtream-series-back').onclick =
1585:             () => openVODProvider(provider);
1586: 
1587:         area.querySelectorAll('[data-xseason]').forEach(button => {
1588:             button.onclick = () => showEpisodes(
1589:                 grouped.get(seasonNumbers[
1590:                     Number(button.dataset.xseason)
1591:                 ]),
1592:                 series,
1593:                 provider
1594:             );
1595:         });
1596: 
1597:         if (!seasonNumbers.length) {
1598:             area.innerHTML +=
1599:                 '<p class="isa-vod-note">Bu dizide bölüm bulunamadı.</p>';
1600:         }
1601: 
1602:     } catch(error) {
1603:         area.innerHTML =
1604:             '<p class="isa-vod-note">Dizi bilgileri alınamadı: ' +
1605:             esc(error.message) + '</p>';
1606:     }
1607: }
1608: 
1609: function seasonNumber(item) {
1610:     const explicit = item.season_number ?? item.season;
1611:     if (explicit !== undefined && explicit !== null &&
1612:         Number.isFinite(Number(explicit))) {
1613:         return Number(explicit);
1614:     }
1615: 
1616:     const title = String(item.title || item.name || '');
1617:     const patterns = [
1618:         /(?:^|\b)(\d+)\s*\.?\s*(?:sezon|season)\b/i,
1619:         /(?:season|sezon)\s*0*(\d+)/i,
1620:         /\bS0*(\d+)E0*\d+\b/i,
1621:         /\b0*(\d+)x0*\d+\b/i
1622:     ];
1623: 
1624:     for (const pattern of patterns) {
1625:         const match = title.match(pattern);
1626:         if (match) return Number(match[1]);
1627:     }
1628:     return 1;
1629: }
1630:

1633: function showSeries(series,provider) {
1634:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1635:     let episodes = series.episodes || [];
1636:     const seasons = series.seasons || [];
1637: 
1638:     if (seasons.length) {
1639:         const grouped = seasons.map((season,index) => ({
1640:             number:season.number||season.season||index+1,
1641:             episodes:season.episodes||[]
1642:         }));
1643: 
1644:         area.innerHTML = `
1645:             <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1646:                 <button class="isa-back" id="isa-series-back"><i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Geri</button>
1647:                 <h2>${esc(series.name)}</h2>
1648:             </div>
1649:             ${series.poster ? `<img src="${esc(series.poster)}" style="width:130px;max-height:180px;object-fit:contain">` : ''}
1650:             <div class="isa-provider-grid">
1651:                 ${grouped.map((season,index) => `
1652:                     <button class="isa-provider-card" data-season="${index}">
1653:                         <i class="fas fa-layer-group" style="font-size:32px;padding:20px"></i>
1654:                         <strong>${esc('Sezon '+season.number)}</strong>
1655:                         <small>${season.episodes.length} bölüm</small>
1656:                     </button>
1657:                 `).join('')}
1658:             </div>
1659:         `;
1660: 
1661:         document.getElementById('isa-series-back').onclick = () => openVODProvider(provider);
1662: 
1663:         area.querySelectorAll('[data-season]').forEach(button => {
1664:             button.onclick = () => showEpisodes(
1665:                 grouped[Number(button.dataset.season)].episodes,
1666:                 series,
1667:                 provider
1668:             );
1669:         });
1670: 
1671:         return;
1672:     }
1673: 
1674:     if (!episodes.length) episodes = series.items || [];
1675: 
1676:     if (!episodes.length) {
1677:         area.innerHTML = `
1678:             <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1679:                 <button class="isa-back" id="isa-series-back">Geri</button>
1680:                 <h2>${esc(series.name)}</h2>
1681:             </div>
1682:             <p class="isa-vod-note">Bu dizinin JSON kaynağında bölüm listesi bulunmuyor.</p>
1683:         `;
1684:         document.getElementById('isa-series-back').onclick = () => openVODProvider(provider);
1685:         return;
1686:     }
1687: 
1688:     const grouped = new Map();
1689: 
1690:     episodes.forEach(episode => {
1691:         const number = seasonNumber(episode);
1692:         if (!grouped.has(number)) grouped.set(number,[]);
1693:         grouped.get(number).push(episode);
1694:     });
1695: 
1696:     if (grouped.size >= 1) {
1697:         area.innerHTML = `
1698:             <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1699:                 <button class="isa-back" id="isa-series-back">Geri</button>
1700:                 <h2>${esc(series.name)}</h2>
1701:             </div>
1702:             <div class="isa-provider-grid">
1703:                 ${Array.from(grouped.entries()).map(([number,eps],index) => `
1704:                     <button class="isa-provider-card" data-season="${index}">
1705:                         <i class="fas fa-layer-group" style="font-size:32px;padding:20px"></i>
1706:                         <strong>Sezon ${number}</strong>
1707:                         <small>${eps.length} bölüm</small>
1708:                     </button>
1709:                 `).join('')}
1710:             </div>
1711:         `;
1712: 
1713:         const values = Array.from(grouped.values());
1714:         document.getElementById('isa-series-back').onclick = () => openVODProvider(provider);
1715: 
1716:         area.querySelectorAll('[data-season]').forEach(button => {
1717:             button.onclick = () => showEpisodes(values[Number(button.dataset.season)],series,provider);
1718:         });
1719:     } else {
1720:         showEpisodes(episodes,series,provider);
1721:     }
1722: }
1723: 
1724: function showEpisodes(episodes,series,provider) {
1725:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1726: 
1727:     area.innerHTML = `
1728:         <div class="isa-vod-toolbar">
1729:             <button class="isa-back" id="isa-episodes-back">
1730:                 <i class="fas fa-arrow-left"></i> Geri
1731:             </button>
1732:             <h2>${esc(series.name)}</h2>
1733:         </div>
1734:         <div class="isa-provider-grid">
1735:             ${episodes.map((episode,index) => `
1736:                 <button class="isa-provider-card" data-episode="${index}">
1737:                     <i class="fas fa-circle-play" style="font-size:35px;padding:20px"></i>
1738:                     <strong>${esc(episode.title||episode.name||('Bölüm '+(index+1)))}</strong>
1739:                     <small>${esc(episode.date||'Bölümü aç')}</small>
1740:                 </button>
1741:             `).join('')}
1742:         </div>
1743:     `;
1744: 
1745:     document.getElementById('isa-episodes-back').onclick = () => showSeries(series,provider);
1746: 
1747:     area.querySelectorAll('[data-episode]').forEach(button => {
1748:         button.onclick = () => openVODItem(episodes[Number(button.dataset.episode)]);
1749:     });
1750: }

1752: 
1753: async function resolveStaticMedia(page, depth=0, visited=new Set()) {
1754:     if (!isHttp(page) || depth > 2 || visited.has(page)) return null;
1755:     visited.add(page);
1756: 
1757:     let html;
1758:     try {
1759:         html = await nativeFetch(page);
1760:     } catch (e) {
1761:         console.warn('VOD sayfası okunamadı:', e);
1762:         return null;
1763:     }
1764: 
1765:     if (!html) return null;
1766: 
1767:     const patterns = [
1768:         /<source[^>]+src=["']([^"']+\.(?:m3u8|mp4|webm)(?:\?[^"']*)?)["']/ig,
1769:         /<video[^>]+src=["']([^"']+\.(?:m3u8|mp4|webm)(?:\?[^"']*)?)["']/ig,
1770:         /(?:file|source|src|hls|playlist)\s*[:=]\s*["']([^"']+\.(?:m3u8|mp4|webm)(?:\?[^"']*)?)["']/ig,
1771:         /<meta[^>]+property=["']og:video["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']/ig
1772:     ];
1773: 
1774:     for (const pattern of patterns) {
1775:         let match;
1776:         while ((match = pattern.exec(html)) !== null) {
1777:             try {
1778:                 const candidate = new URL(
1779:                     match[1].replace(/&amp;/g,'&'),
1780:                     page
1781:                 ).href;
1782: 
1783:                 if (isDirectMedia(candidate)) {
1784:                     return candidate;
1785:                 }
1786:             } catch (e) {}
1787:         }
1788:     }
1789: 
1790:     // Yalnızca sayfada açıkça belirtilmiş iframe adreslerini kontrol et.
1791:     const iframePattern = /<iframe[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/ig;
1792:     let iframe;
1793: 
1794:     while ((iframe = iframePattern.exec(html)) !== null) {
1795:         try {
1796:             const child = new URL(iframe[1],page).href;
1797:             if (!isHttp(child)) continue;
1798: 
1799:             const found = await resolveStaticMedia(
1800:                 child,
1801:                 depth+1,
1802:                 visited
1803:             );
1804: 
1805:             if (found) return found;
1806:         } catch (e) {}
1807:     }
1808: 
1809:     return null;
1810: }

1812: async function openVODItem(item) {
1813:     const candidates = [
1814:         item.videoUrl,
1815:         item.video_url,
1816:         item.playUrl,
1817:         item.stream,
1818:         item.file,
1819:         item.url,
1820:         item.link,
1821:         item.embedUrl
1822:     ].filter(Boolean);
1823: 
1824:     const media = candidates.find(isDirectMedia);
1825: 
1826:     if (media) {
1827:         play({
1828:             name:item.title||item.name||'İSA Flix',
1829:             url:media,
1830:             logo:item.poster||item.logo||'',
1831:             group:'İSA Flix',
1832:             headers:item.headers||{}
1833:         },[],0);
1834:         return;
1835:     }
1836: 
1837:     const page = candidates.find(isHttp);
1838: 
1839:     if (!page) {
1840:         alert('Bu içerik için geçerli bir bağlantı bulunamadı.');
1841:         return;
1842:     }
1843: 
1844:     const area = document.getElementById('isa-vod-items');
1845:     const note = document.createElement('p');
1846:     note.className = 'isa-vod-note';
1847:     note.textContent = 'Video kaynağı aranıyor...';
1848:     area.prepend(note);
1849: 
1850:     try {
1851:         const resolved = await resolveStaticMedia(page);
1852: 
1853:         note.remove();
1854: 
1855:         if (!resolved) {
1856:             alert(
1857:                 'Bu kaynağın sayfasında doğrudan ve erişilebilir ' +
1858:                 'bir video adresi bulunamadı. Kaynak dinamik oynatıcı ' +
1859:                 'veya erişim koruması kullanıyor olabilir.'
1860:             );
1861:             return;
1862:         }
1863: 
1864:         play({
1865:             name:item.title||item.name||'İSA Flix',
1866:             url:resolved,
1867:             logo:item.poster||item.logo||'',
1868:             group:'İSA Flix',
1869:             headers:{
1870:                 ...(item.headers||{}),
1871:                 Referer:page
1872:             }
1873:         },[],0);
1874: 
1875:     } catch (error) {
1876:         note.remove();
1877:         console.error('VOD çözümleme hatası:',error);
1878:         alert('Video kaynağı çözümlenemedi: '+error.message);
1879:     }
1880: }

1906:

1908:         alert('Bu içerik için geçerli bağlantı bulunamadı.');

1943: async function refreshAll(forceRefresh=false) {
1944:     const status = document.getElementById('isa-live-status');
1945:     status.textContent = forceRefresh
1946:         ? 'Kaynaklar güncelleniyor...'
1947:         : 'Önbellek ve kayıtlı listeler yükleniyor...';
1948: 
1949:     let live = [];
1950:     let user = [];
1951: 
1952:     try {
1953:         live = await loadLiveChannels(forceRefresh);
1954:     } catch(error) {
1955:         console.error('kaynak TV:',error);
1956:     }
1957: 
1958:     try {
1959:         user = await loadUserLists(forceRefresh);
1960:     } catch(error) {
1961:         console.error('Kullanıcı listeleri:',error);
1962:     }
1963: 
1964:     channels = [];
1965:     for (const channel of live) channels.push(channel);
1966:     for (const channel of user) channels.push(channel);
1967:     renderChannels();
1968:     renderSavedLists();
1969: 
1970:     status.textContent = '';
1971:     playlists.forEach(p => { if (p.type === 'xtream' && !p.lastCount) p.lastCount = user.length; });
1972:     renderSavedLists();
1973: 
1974:     // Film ve dizi panelleri kaynakları hesap bazında ayrı tutar.
1975:     renderMediaPanel('film');
1976:     renderMediaPanel('series');
1977:     // İSA Flix bu sürümde bilerek boş bırakılmıştır.
1978: }

2053: async function addXtreamSource(host,user,pass) {
2054:     const source = {
2055:         host,
2056:         username:user,
2057:         password:pass
2058:     };
2059: 
2060:     sourceLoading(true, 'Xtream hesabı kontrol ediliyor...');
2061: 
2062:     const auth = await xtreamAPI(source, 'get_live_categories');
2063: 
2064:     if (!Array.isArray(auth)) {
2065:         throw new Error('Xtream API yanıtı geçersiz.');
2066:     }
2067: 
2068:     playlists.push({
2069:         ...source,
2070:         type:'xtream',
2071:         name:nextSourceName('xtream'),
2072:         text:''
2073:     });
2074: 
2075:     saveLists();
2076:     await refreshAll(false);
2077: }

2079: async function addSource() {
2080:     const type = document.getElementById('isa-source-type').value;
2081:     if (!type) {
2082:         alert('Önce kaynak türünü seçin.');
2083:         return;
2084:     }
2085: 
2086:     sourceLoading(true, 'Kaynak kontrol ediliyor...');
2087: 
2088:     try {
2089:         if (type === 'm3u') {
2090:             const url = document.getElementById(
2091:                 'isa-new-m3u-url'
2092:             ).value.trim();
2093: 
2094:             if (!isHttp(url)) {
2095:                 throw new Error('Geçerli HTTP/HTTPS M3U adresi girin.');
2096:             }
2097: 
2098:             await addM3UUrl(url);
2099: 
2100:         } else if (type === 'xtream') {
2101:             const hostInput = document.getElementById('isa-xt-host').value;
2102:             const user = document.getElementById('isa-xt-user').value.trim();
2103:             const pass = document.getElementById('isa-xt-pass').value;
2104: 
2105:             if (!hostInput.trim() || !user || !pass) {
2106:                 throw new Error('Host, kullanıcı adı ve şifre zorunludur.');
2107:             }
2108: 
2109:             let parsed = sourceHost(hostInput);
2110: 
2111:             if (!parsed.hasPort) {
2112:                 const port = document.getElementById('isa-xt-port').value.trim();
2113:                 if (!port || !/^\d{1,5}$/.test(port) ||
2114:                     Number(port) < 1 || Number(port) > 65535) {
2115:                     throw new Error('Geçerli bir port girin.');
2116:                 }
2117: 
2118:                 parsed.host = parsed.host.replace(/\/+$/, '') +
2119:                     ':' + port;
2120:             }
2121: 
2122:             await addXtreamSource(parsed.host,user,pass);
2123: 
2124:         } else if (type === 'mag') {
2125:             const host = sourceHost(
2126:                 document.getElementById('isa-mag-host').value
2127:             ).host;
2128: 
2129:             const mac = document.getElementById(
2130:                 'isa-mag-mac'
2131:             ).value.trim().toUpperCase();
2132: 
2133:             if (!/^([0-9A-F]{2}:){5}[0-9A-F]{2}$/.test(mac)) {
2134:                 throw new Error('MAC adresini 00:1A:79:XX:XX:XX biçiminde girin.');
2135:             }
2136: 
2137:             const source = {
2138:                 host,
2139:                 mac,
2140:                 type:'mag',
2141:                 name:nextSourceName('mag')
2142:             };
2143: 
2144:             await loadMagChannels(source);
2145: 
2146:             playlists.push(source);
2147:             saveLists();
2148:             await refreshAll(false);
2149:         }
2150: 
2151:         document.getElementById('isa-upload-status').textContent =
2152:             'Kaynak başarıyla eklendi.';
2153: 
2154:         document.getElementById('isa-source-type').value = '';
2155:         renderSourceFields();
2156:         const form=document.getElementById('isa-source-form'); if(form) form.hidden=true;
2157:         const toggle=document.getElementById('isa-toggle-source-form'); if(toggle) toggle.textContent='＋ Liste ekle';
2158: 
2159:     } catch(error) {
2160:         document.getElementById('isa-upload-status').textContent =
2161:             error.message || 'Kaynak eklenemedi.';
2162:         console.error(error);
2163:     } finally {
2164:         sourceLoading(false);
2165:     }
2166: }

2198:                 'M3U listesi eklendi.';

2233:                 targetPanel.classList.add('active');
2234:                 targetPanel.style.display = 'block';

2239:             if (button.dataset.tab === 'isa-films-panel') renderMediaPanel('film');
2240:             if (button.dataset.tab === 'isa-series-panel') renderMediaPanel('series');
2241:         };
2242:     });

2249: function mediaSourceKey(source) { return [source.type,source.host||source.url||source.name||'',source.username||'',source.name||''].join('|'); }
2250: async function renderMediaPanel(kind) {
2251:     const root = document.getElementById(
2252:         kind === 'film' ? 'isa-films-content' : 'isa-series-content'
2253:     );
2254:     if (!root) return;
2255: 
2256:     const source = playlists.length ? playlists[playlists.length - 1] : null;
2257: 
2258:     if (!source) {
2259:         root.innerHTML = '<p class="isa-empty-state">Henüz giriş yapılmış bir kaynak bulunamadı.</p>';
2260:         return;
2261:     }
2262: 
2263:     if (!['xtream', 'file', 'url', 'm3u-url'].includes(source.type)) {
2264:         root.innerHTML = '<p class="isa-empty-state">Aktif hesap film ve dizi içeriklerini desteklemiyor.</p>';
2265:         return;
2266:     }
2267: 
2268:     await openMediaSource(source, kind, root);
2269: }

2271: function renderMediaGroups(groups,source,kind,root) {
2272:     root.innerHTML = `<div class="isa-vod-toolbar"><h2>${esc(source.name||'Liste')}</h2></div><div class="isa-provider-grid">${Array.from(groups.keys()).map((g,i)=>`<button class="isa-provider-card" data-media-cat="${i}"><i class="fas ${kind==='film'?'fa-folder-open':'fa-folder'}"></i><strong>${esc(cleanGroupTitle(g))}</strong><small>${groups.get(g).length} içerik</small></button>`).join('')}</div>${groups.size?'':'<p class="isa-vod-note">Bu kaynakta bu türde içerik bulunamadı.</p>'}`;
2273:     const names = Array.from(groups.keys());
2274:     root.querySelectorAll('[data-media-cat]').forEach(b =>
2275:         b.onclick = () => renderMediaItems(
2276:             groups.get(names[Number(b.dataset.mediaCat)]),
2277:             kind, source, root, names[Number(b.dataset.mediaCat)]
2278:         )
2279:     );
2280: }
